- **evidence (SPEC_synthetic_minimum_network §17, SEAM-SCOPED ZWD SERIALIZATION entry, 2026-09-01, single build `87d8629c…`):** (a) 12 h continuous flagship-verbatim run BYTE-IDENTICAL (products + closed_loop + network_zwd, sha256-equal) to `runs/camp60wu_quiet12h_r2q3` cross-build; (w') capture-LIVE re-run of the q6wsnap pattern byte-identical to the old-build run with the final snapshot carrying 60 Z rows under the UNCHANGED fingerprint `35860c5f45b8a004`; (c') `runs/camp60wu_q6warm_szv` warm resume: 60/60 Z rows restored + ARMED, 28 800 folds = exactly 60×480, RETIRED on schedule, ZWD no-restart (h6 truth rms 0.124 vs cold 0.390, beats the (cz) full-B9 reference's 0.172 AND the (z) ceiling's 0.169 — healthier seed values + honest capture σ 4-8 mm vs the double-count-tightened 1-1.5 mm), settled h9-12 consumer 0.134 m rms ≈≤ flagship 0.143 (no-Z warm was 0.278: the whole settled gap recovered), retirement consumer-free (h11 0.119 vs (cz) still-injecting 0.134), ZWD vf 0.206 conservative vs (cz) 1.232 optimistic. Post-retirement ZWD truth drift h10→h11 (0.141→0.199) is the restored zero-q AMBIGUITY carries' invariant-#1 drag (measured 0.173 at h11 on the no-Z warm arm with no seam at all) — owned by the warm-resume card, not this knob.
- **interlock:** Deliberately NOT in the warm-resume fingerprint — the EIGHTH forward-only response-policy exemption (`networkSnapshotFingerprint` comment block): it changes what a resumed run DOES with restored Z rows, re-references nothing, changes what no serialized state MEANS, and a token would outlaw the same-snapshot cold/warm A/B its validation used. Inert without `state_snapshot_path` (warned) and rides the carry_warm gate, so inert without `phase_leveling` (warned) and until warmup on conv-gated SH lineages. With `carry_zwd` also on, B9 subsumes the window (info-logged; counters still tick). Uses `network.zwd_carry_q` (fingerprinted, default 1e-9) for the RW inflation. Its own countdown is never serialized: each resume re-arms a full horizon — a mid-window re-resume is a NEW seam by design. Unit contract: tests/test_zwd_seam_reseed_config.cpp.
- **field scope note:**  FIELD SCOPE NOTE (SPEC_event_harness s21.14, 2026-09-13): probed on the field golden's warm resume (runs/evseed_field6h_v5{,raw} ep0604, legs rs6_{raw,null}_both_nozwd vs _both) with the branch, datums, emit layer and station layer all restored: OFF improves ZWD leg-seed (pooled p50 0.64 -> 0.41 m, p95 5.0 -> 2.6), receiver clocks at +1 (0.93 -> 0.75 m), the raw PB tail at +30 and the pooled clock-contrast p95, but worsens the SH a00 residual (+0.43 -> +0.73 TECU, no longer decaying), the +2 clock contrast (0.29 -> 0.44 m), the code-bias level (0.46 -> 0.50) and the D5 trips at +1 (38 -> 53); the rx-CB gate share and the stateful consumer's seam behaviour are unchanged either way.  MIXED on the field (whose ZWD channel is the datum sink, not a physical ZWD): the harness default stays armed; the decision is deferred to RS-7 (the dense shared border), whose restore carries the ZWD / DCB / clock split this knob interacts with.
- **RS-10 field verdict (SPEC_event_harness s21.21, 2026-09-13):** OFF on any lineage that does not CARRY the ZWD; regime narrowed to `warm and (on("network.phase_leveling_local_block") or on("network.carry_zwd"))`.  On the field golden the Z rows are exact (0.0000 m, absolute frame) but the ZWD is a per-epoch white sink (own step 0.107 m p50): the B9 prior at q 1e-9 is ~4000x too stiff (zwd prior sigma0 11.2, refused every epoch) and from +2 the harvest makes it the falsified diagonal ZWD carry for 480 epochs (ZWD leg-seed 0.29 -> 1.34 m by +60; satellite-clock contrast p95 10.3 vs 5.4 without it); the receiver-clock re-split at +1 is the same event (r = -0.90).  scripts/event_leg.py arms it only on carrying lineages.
### `network.zwd_seam_reseed_q`
- **default:** -1.0 at src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (jval "network.zwd_seam_reseed_q", beside zwd_seam_reseed; throw on 0 or negative-when-set); resolved once as NetworkKnobs::zwdQEff(seam_live) (include/app/NetworkRunContext.hpp) and read at both B9 fold sites (src/core/app/ApplicationNetworkAssembly.cpp) and the harvest census; merged main 9cf83b3 (worker e2bf262).  — **regime:** `warm and on("network.zwd_seam_reseed")`.  **RECOMMEND_SCOPED.**  The ZWD seam reseed's OWN stiffness (m^2/s): the process-noise term of the B9 seam priors (var + q*gap) and of their prior-residual census inside the seam window only; default -1 = inherit network.zwd_carry_q (byte-identical); must be > 0 when set.  NOT in the warm-resume fingerprint (a resume response policy) - the reseed borrowed zwd_carry_q, which IS fingerprinted, so its stiffness could never be varied on a resume leg before this knob.  Arm on the RESUME leg together with zwd_seam_reseed.
- **evidence:** RS-10b (SPEC_event_harness s21.21, 2026-09-13; exe E5CC8A52 on main 25d4ea3+; legs rs10b_null_{on,q4,q3,q2} from evseed_field6h_v7 ep0604, single knob each, all warm-resumed under the unchanged fingerprint 545b8bdd2b638231; the ON reference reproduces rs9_null_lin byte-for-byte): the seam reseed's stiffness is now tunable on a resume leg.  Bracket vs the reseed-OFF arm rs10_null_nozwd: at the inherited 1e-9 the seam prior is over-stiff (sigma0 p50 11.2, 215 of 3721 folds dropped by the harvest's var guard, ZWD leg-seed p50 1.34 m and satellite-clock contrast p95 10.3 at +60; cold in-run consumer k5-32 3D 8.8 m); q 3.8e-4: sigma0 1.32, ZWD +60 0.90, contrast p95 4.0, cold consumer 6.4 m / |up| 2.3 (the best consumer arm); q 2.5e-3: sigma0 0.60, ZWD +60 0.618 (bar 0.60, OFF 0.584 - the one producer-side miss, 3 %), contrast p95 4.05, tails ZWD max 9.9 -> 7.3 m / rx-clock max 17.1 -> 12.6, cold consumer 7.3 m / |up| k33-60 0.97 (best of five); q 1.7e-2: sigma0 0.24, all folds, ZWD +60 0.47 (better than OFF), contrast p95 4.9, tails at OFF's level (3.25 / 5.9) but the cold consumer 12.2 m / |up| 9.8 (broken).  The crossover lies in (2.5e-3, 1.7e-2); no q held both pre-registered ends, so by the pre-registration the tie-break is the stateful-consumer splice, indifferent to the reseed (s21.14) => the reseed stays OFF on lineages that carry no ZWD; q 2.5e-3 is the re-arm candidate on record, re-tested on the v8 seed.
- **interlock:** Meaningful only with network.zwd_seam_reseed; the continuous carry path (carry_zwd) keeps zwd_carry_q.
- **armed when:** `on("network.zwd_seam_reseed")`  every leg that arms the reseed on a lineage carrying no ZWD memory - scripts/event_leg.py sets it with the reseed (no q holds both ends yet; the reseed stays OFF there); carrying lineages inherit zwd_carry_q.

### `network.cb_kaula_prior`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:4323-4324 (false)  — **regime:** GPS+Gal network producer with `estimate_sat_code_bias` on (INERT without it — the prior folds onto registered satellite code-bias columns). Validated at 63 sta, DOY-129, 30 s, both on a 150-epoch leg and a full 12 h. NOT validated GPS-only (GPS-only does not exhibit the deficiency: its graph stays connected through universally-tracked channels with strong code), and NOT with BeiDou in the roster.
- **evidence:** project_network_ssystem_dia_assessment. The FT63 GPS+Gal signature is `observed 4 (near-)zero eigenvalue(s) ... declared S-basis dimension is 2` — a second constellation adds per-satellite channel-family phase-bias↔slant-iono trade directions the structural datum does not declare. Card PA's drop-and-heal anchors PB columns only and cannot see the CODE-dominated orphan direction. KP folds a loose physical-bound prior (satellite OSBs are bounded at tens of ns — real information, not a fudge) onto every admitted OSB column, lifting BOTH the orphan and the trades above the rank cutoff. Full 12 h leg `runs/ft63_gg12h_sbckp`: **1440/1440 emitted, 0 withheld, 0 PA heals**, vs the baseline's 1433 emitted / 7 gate-withheld / PA firing on 41% of epochs. Re-confirmed same-build on a 150-epoch A/B over the TOW 263250-263370 withhold cluster (`runs/sbcfix_{off,on}`, 2026-08-03): OFF **145/150** with 5 gate withholds and 600 `phase_heal` events; ON **150/150, 0 withheld, 0 heals**. Mechanism identified from the heal attribution: healed spans 0.350774/0.333165/0.341799 on E08 L5X/L7X/L8X match μ(E5a):μ(E5b):μ(E5) to five decimals.
- **cost:** post-fit code σ̂₀ rose **4.63 → 5.12 (+10.5%)** across that A/B on an identical measurement set (9587 rows). The prior mildly pulls OSB columns toward 0, so this buys emission integrity at a measurable cost in code-residual fit. A consumer-replay `total_IF` grade is still owed and is the honest arbiter of net product quality.
- **interlock:** INERT unless `network.estimate_sat_code_bias`. Its σ is load-bearing and must travel with it — see `network.cb_kaula_sigma_m`; at the 10 m default the producer's own K2 anti-fragility check WARNS. Travels with `network.sbasis_complete` as the GPS+Gal datum recipe: KP does the lifting and SBC is the armed net for any epoch KP's lift misses (with KP off, SBC alone carried 145/150 and completed 108 epochs). NOT a member of the warm-resume fingerprint (`NetworkRunContext.cpp`), so arming it mid-chain on a warm resume changes the solve **without** forcing a cold start or a hard abort — arm it in a fresh lineage.
### `network.cb_kaula_sigma_m`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:4326-4327 (10.0)  — **regime:** Set **3.0** wherever `cb_kaula_prior` is armed. Same envelope as that knob. INERT unless it is on.
- **evidence:** project_network_ssystem_dia_assessment. The K2 anti-fragility rule requires the prior's information 1/σ² to sit ≥ 2 decades ABOVE the armed rank-gate cutoff — the CB-ADM card rejected a loose prior whose information sat AT the cutoff, where it either trips the gate or survives by coincidence. On this config the 10 m default gives only **1.6 decades** and the producer WARNS; **σ=3 m gives 2.7 decades** (`prior info 1.111e-01 vs rank-gate cutoff 2.472e-04`, logged at the first fold in `runs/sbcfix_on`) and is what the passing 12 h leg used.
- **interlock:** INERT unless `network.cb_kaula_prior`. Do not copy 3.0 to another constellation set or station count without re-reading that config's own rank-gate cutoff from `network_estimability.csv` — the margin is a ratio against a cutoff that moves with the constellation mix, exactly like `network.rank_gate_tol`.
### `network.clock_datum_broadcast_anchored`
- **default:** false — grep `network_clock_datum_broadcast_anchored_` in `GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp` (line pins drift; the old :1992 pin is stale)  — **regime:** TWO scoped regimes, one per a-priori source. (1) Real-time self-sufficient service on apriori_source=brdc_rts, where the requirement is an absolute clock frame that does not wander on rise/set — measured NEUTRAL for consumer accuracy at 305 sta; do not arm it expecting a settled positional win. (2) **Ignition/bootstrap service windows (sim-measured 2026-08-31): the biggest first-half consumer win on the 60-sta truth bed** — arm it when the mission window is the first ~6 h of a cold producer; it is a settled-state REGRESSION, so not a flagship posture.
- **evidence (field, 305 sta, brdc_rts):** project_network_falsified_approaches.md: 'Kills redistribution wander (set-event step -0.0003 vs s2z +0.334 m) but consumer U identical (-5.43), 0 fixes, churn unchanged: bca stabilizes clock COMMON-MODE which the consumer white-noise clock absorbs. Valid for absolute RT clock frame.' Design correction embedded in the loader: 'realized common-mode eigenvalue is 1/sigma^2 (NOT ~N/sigma^2) — sigma >~60 m at 305 sta drops below the rank cutoff -> gate throws -> SILENT empty product'.
- **evidence (sim, 60-sta quiet wu93 12 h truth bed, 2026-08-31 — `runs/camp60wu_quiet12h_r2q3bca` vs `_r2q3`, single-knob, SPEC_synthetic_minimum_network §17):** sp3 + explicit σ=3.0 m (the EXTERNAL-CLOCK HOLD; on this bed the a-priori IS truth, so the C channel is partly by-construction and the graded channels are consumer + applied products). **Ignition: consumer snap 4.37 → 1.01 h, h0-h5 hourly 3D rms 2-4× better, worst excursion 1.06 → 0.51 m, ZWD h3 0.31 → 0.17.** **Settled: consumer 3D rms 0.204 → 0.280 m (p50 0.128 → 0.253, floor wanders 0.17-0.38 and never reaches r2q3's 0.11-0.13); cb p50 2.8 → 4.6 cm (+67 %, the SAME bill as sum-to-zero) with vf ×6.8; ZWD stalls at a 0.12-0.18 rms floor (h11 0.026 → 0.138) instead of converging — the per-epoch anchor is an information FLOOR the settled network cannot average past.** Third instance of the S0 lesson: evicting the level from the sat-clock channel — even into a truth-quality frame — sends it to channels the consumer APPLIES. Structure: pivot seam gone exactly as s0 (cta_decouple 214 → 15, pivot handovers 4 → 0, pivot rows 0); per-sat settled clock tails are fixture-common (same worst sats both arms; anchor-vs-IF-lump tension corr +0.06 ≈ nil). ⚠ QC side effect: post-fit T3/T4 flag volume collapses 11110 → 17 / 699 → 2 on identical 1.3 M opportunities — consistent with pivot-frame re-referencing churn being the flag source, but the tier's operating point moves ~650×: **re-verify MDB on a faulted bed before trusting T3/T4 under any anchor-free clock datum.** Derived follow-up (not built): σ-schedule (bind in ignition, release once data information overtakes) — or get the same first half from snapshot warm-start with no settled tax.
- **interlock:** Under apriori_source != brdc_rts (i.e. sp3), HARD ERROR unless network.apriori_clock_anchor_sigma_m is EXPLICIT and > 0 (the sp3 auto resolution is initial_std.sat_clock ≈ 1000 m — non-binding, gauge unrealized, every epoch withheld; the loader banner is 'EXTERNAL-CLOCK HOLD ON', with the do-not-grade-against-the-anchor-product warning). The old ':2355-2361 HARD ERROR if != brdc_rts' pin described a superseded loader. HARD ERROR with clock_datum_sum_to_zero (mutually exclusive gauge realizations). Resolved σ hard-gated to (0,100) m, warns above 30 m. Silently makes sticky_pivot / pivot_vanish_return_signal INERT and ignores clock_datum_satellite. Datum toggle — INSIDE the warm-resume fingerprint (fresh convergence only). Composes with clock_temporal_anchor (Q3) with no loader interlock: both fold information on the same common mode (Q3's §2.1 'competes with the S-basis datum' banner applies); the r2q3bca arm ran both — a bca-without-Q3 decomposition has NOT been run.
### `network.clock_datum_sum_to_zero`
- **⚠ THIS ROW IS THE SINGLE ENTRY FOR THIS KNOB.** It absorbed the former duplicate Tier-4 KEEP_OFF entry (2026-08-15): the ledger carried the knob TWICE, in two tiers, with contradictory verdicts and two different loader line numbers. Both evidence sets survive here — they are compatible (sim PASS on the gauge property, field DOES-NOT-GRADUATE on the consumer trade, and FALSIFIED as the fix for the 20:47 CB excursion). ✅ **SYNCED 2026-08-15 (serial cleanup pass): the KEEP_OFF object was dropped from `docs/CONFIG_promotion_ledger.json`**, which now carries the single RECOMMEND_SCOPED row that `config_posture_check.py --verify-loader` reads — the MISSING-from-loader report for this knob should clear.
- **default:** false — `jval<bool>(json, "network.clock_datum_sum_to_zero", "network_clock_datum_sum_to_zero", false)` in `GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp` (:4515-4517 as of 2026-08-15 — **grep the symbol, per the standing line-pin rule**: this pin has already drifted three times, `:1982` → `:4224` → `:4420-4422` → `:4515-4517`, the last shift from a concurrent uncommitted workstream in the same TU).  — **regime:** Clock-product consumers that integrate the ABSOLUTE clock level (timing, inter-provider comparison) and every internal vf/URA channel — the wire common level is where the datum receiver's clock-estimation error lands 1:1 under the legacy pivot-satellite emit. A PPP consumer estimates its own clock and does NOT benefit (measured: cdC consumer unchanged-to-worse). Sim-validated 2026-08-11 (quiet 50-sta fixture); field-validated for WIRING 2026-08-07 and jump-count-A/B'd at 12 h 2026-08-08 — **it does NOT graduate; the pin datum stays the default.**
- **evidence (field, 12 h A/B — the actual Edge-2 verdict; §A2 F10 + F13):** Wiring smoke first (1 h leveled pair `runs/evt_pl_clk{pin,s2z}`, configs `_ext63_1h_evt_pl_clk{pin,s2z}.json` which carry the pre-registered predictions and which the 12 h continuation extends directly, both 121/121 emitted, 0 withheld): every predicted loader interlock fired (PIN arm sticky clock-pivot LIVE with no INERT warn; S2Z arm shows the SUM-TO-ZERO banner, the sticky_pivot INERT warn and the datum-graph declared-d=0 semantics warn); clock census CLEAN on both (0 common-mode steps > 0.1 m, 0 per-sat outliers > 0.5 m); 0 pivot/handover/set-change events in either arm (quiet hour), 2 R records both. Then 12 h (`runs/evt_pl_clk{pin,s2z}12`, 1440/1440 each, + replays `runs/replay_12h_clk{pin,s2z}`): producer census CLEAN in BOTH datums; pin exactly 1 `datum_changed`, s2z 0 as predicted — the feared s2z membership-redistribution wander never crossed the census floor; PB churn posture-invariant (202 vs 195 key-steps, same events, same epochs); 68 R records both. CONSUMER (WTZZ replays, H-forwarding live 1440/1440 both): s2z takes **106 resets vs 124 (−15 %)** and ELIMINATES the pin arm's excursion tails (60.9 m @h2 / 28.8 @h3 → worst hour 19.1) but pays a steadily HIGHER median through h1-h7 (~9 m vs ~3-4 m) — the redistribution expressed as a slow consumer bias instead of steps. ⚠ The CLAUDE.md amendment-5 **13-jumps/12 h figure did NOT reproduce here — it belongs to the FT63/golden lineage** (measured directly by the internal run, §A2 F15).
- **evidence (falsified as the fix — retain, this is a different question):** project_s2z_realization_ab.md, CLOSED WITH DATA against a pre-registered decision rule: 'Verdict H2: the 20:47 excursion still ratchets into the wire under s2z, redistributed across 25/32 sats (worst +28.5 m EOD, never re-tracks) instead of concentrating +42.7 m on the pivot ... Pin + Phase-2 guards posture stands; no default flips. Price of redistribution: the datum-pinned pair (arm B) transiently paid 2.4x the reference — 34.6 vs 14.6 m at hour 21.' It also breaks an armed guard: 'What the gauge DESTROYS is the one-sided discriminator: max/med = 1.23 under the pin (the common-mode fingerprint that predicts harm) vs 32.6 under s2z, indistinguishable from a benign two-sided event.' Retained as a valid S-transform feature; falsified as the fix.
- **evidence (sim, the gauge property):** SPEC_synthetic_minimum_network.md §9.8. First validation PASS: wire mean-over-sats ≡ 0.0000 exactly every epoch (constraint holds bit-for-bit); settled common-mode wander 0.193 → 0.036 m at a fixed POLAR datum station (S01, lat −68.6°) and 0.024 → 0.014 m at an equatorial one; availability unchanged (232/240 emitted, 8 A4 withholds, identical to control); rank gate passes (declared_dimension 1 → 0, self-consistently — the §5 caveat that the gate cannot distinguish realizations applies). Companion finding, same arm matrix: the datum-free CONTRAST also moves 2.2× with the pivot's geometry (0.966 → 0.443 m polar → equatorial), so PIVOT ELECTION is the consumer-facing lever while sum-to-zero is the wire-level one; combined arm (equatorial + Σ=0) is best on every channel (C rms 0.286 m, wander 0.014 m, consumer 3D 0.208 m).
- **interlock:** Datum toggle — INSIDE the warm-resume fingerprint (fresh convergence only, never flip mid-chain). Makes sticky_pivot / pivot_vanish_return_signal / clock_datum_satellite INERT (no pivot satellite, no handover seam). Deliberately NOT armed by service_mode. The arms differ in the SHAPE of the code-bias error — ON a bias-like level the sum-to-zero datum does not hold at exactly, OFF a heavy tail on two signals — and the consumer prefers ON by a third.  **This also CLOSES `SPEC_family_gf_datum` §2.1's owed work**: the co-armed "inconsistent gauge" pair (time-linked s2z RHS + the primary GF code-bias pin, which BOTH goldens arm and the loader WARNs about every run) injects **no measurable tension on this bed** — keep the knob armed, keep the WARN informational.  Closure scope: SIM only, one bed, and §2.1's rank-1 dependency is exact only where the s2z veteran set matches the pinned set; the FIELD A/B is not run. ### `network.cycle_slip.datum_station_detect`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (loadNetworkSlipDetectors_, false)  — **regime:** LEGACY single-pivot phase datum with phase_leveling + mw_detect (loader hard-disables without mw_detect; runtime-inert under any no-pivot datum — prioritized/ensemble/eq34 already run slip loops from i_lo=0). Validated on the 50-sta synthetic fault fixture only (2026-08-11); no field arm yet.
- **evidence:** SPEC_synthetic_minimum_network.md §9.7.10. Closes the §9.7.4 proven blind spot (the datum station has NO slip detection under the legacy datum, and an undetected slip there re-references every emitted phase bias of that satellite with no record). Single-knob A/B on the pivotswap fault fixture (settled half): C rms 1.657 → 0.485 m (3.4×), C max 18.14 → 1.44 m, ZWD rms 1.18 → 0.69 m, spurious CLK_STEP marks 12 → 1, emitted_contaminated 27 → 0, A4 withholds UNCHANGED 14/14 — i.e. zero availability cost, vs 97/240 withheld for the phase_datum_prioritized alternative (§9.7.7). OFF path proven bit-identical (products + all decision traces) against the retained mwblind2 run. Response is a two-edge network re-reference (invariant #7): datum_slip queue edge (PB carry hard skip-stash) + datum_reref apply edge (all-station amb reseed aligned across withholds, R-record disc mark, run-end counters).
- **interlock:** Requires mw_detect (its detection vehicle; parsed AFTER the invariant-#1 auto-arm so phase_leveling alone satisfies it). Known cost: MW ignition candidates at the pivot cause a spurious re-reference at epoch ~2 (one G12 event on the fixture, marked honestly); field-inert during ignition, and bounded by the same §9.7.5 mid-arc MW silence as everywhere — LLI is the demonstrated live catcher. Pivot remains outside post-fit RAIM cells and has no IF-TD detector (named follow-ups). Warm resume drops a pending re-reference queue (same transient class as refsat_mark_sats).
### `network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_gate`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:1315  — **regime:** Card R. WARM VALIDATION LANDED 2026-09-08 (sim, SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount.md 6.14.4): 2 h / 240 ep / 60 sta golden A/B, one knob differing -- acting refsat refloats 546 -> 0, steady-state rate 0.504 -> 0.000 per epoch, distinct arcs hit 137 -> 0, MW/WL events 142 = 142, pool arm 0 flags both arms, sigma0 / sigma0_pf unchanged; run-end counter 1090 legacy candidates -> 118 fired (948 suppressed by the scale/coherence test), agreeing with the event file to 1.9%. All 118 survivors are cold-era diagnostic at ep 1 and 3 -- the cold prior degenerating to the legacy floor by design -- then silent for 237 consecutive epochs. Prior evidence was an 11-epoch COLD smoke on ONE config (150 sta, GPS-only, all-freq/all-channel), which exercises mostly the cold prior. Cold-start caveat is a known, expected behaviour, not a regression. Note the OFF-arm false rate is itself fixture-dependent: the sim bed is 5x noisier than a real 300-sta trace at matched elevation, so the legacy fixed 0.5*lambda_IF bar sits at 3.0 sigma there and ~15 sigma on the field.
- **evidence:** docs/SPEC_slip_qc_hardening.md §8.4.4.1 (independent grader, does not read producer counters): raw-confirmed rate 'golden (OFF) 71% -> armed (ON) 90%'; unsupported flags '14 — JPLM/G10 x9, MKEA/G10 x5' -> '4'; pool arm untouched (32 -> 33 flags on 8717 -> 8727 samples); the real OWMG G31 re-lock 'MET — survives (TOW 259320, 259380) ... on the new station-event path'. §8.5: 'the empirical dispersion EWMA only reaches full weight after ~shrink_kappa samples; a 10-epoch run exercises mostly the cold prior.' project_ifphase_mw_slip_agreement.md: 'Consequence to expect, not a regression: the first ~10 epochs of any cold run still run on the physical-floor arm and can emit a few unsupported refsat fires.'
- **interlock:** Loader FORCES it false with a warn if network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td is off (:1369-1374). Config default ref_max_consecutive=1 deliberately differs from the header default 2 in RefGateParams (measured: false family 14->6 at 2, 14->4 at 1) — a drift-guard-tested divergence, do not 'fix' it.
### `network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_warmup_abstain`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (if_phase_td_ref_warmup_abstain, false)  — **regime:** SS6.14.6 item 1.  Abstain until the empirical scale holds shrink_kappa samples instead of falling back to the legacy fixed floor while cold.  SIM-ONLY, one 2 h arm.  Filed UNVALIDATED not because the mechanism is in doubt but because the bed cannot test the thing that matters: the quiet fixture injects 0 slips, so an arm can only show the gate firing LESS, which a gate that never fires would also show.  Promotion needs the SS6.14.6.4 fault arm (--signal-mode mw-blind --elev-window 60 90) to show a real reference slip still caught.
- **evidence:** 2 h / 240 ep / 60 sta golden A/B (runs/ifg_off vs runs/ifg_w, one knob differing, same exe): refsat diagnostic fires 118 -> 0, tier T1r 28680/118 -> 28680/0, 284 warm-up abstentions counted, MW/WL events 142 = 142, pool arm T1 0 flags both arms, ACTING refloats 0 in both.  OFF control byte-identical to runs/ifsc_new on solution/events/flag_rates/if_slip.  MOTIVATION (SS6.14.4): all 118 fires Card R leaves are at epochs 1 and 3 and every one is action=diagnostic, because carry_warm is false there and a cold detection cannot refloat.  CAVEAT, do not oversell: the ACTING path was already 0 under Card R alone, so this removes 118 unactionable events and a legacy-floor fallback, NOT refloat damage.
- **interlock:** Loader forces it false with a warn if .if_phase_td_ref_gate is off (evaluateRefGate is the only reader and is only reached when the hardened gate is armed).  Threshold is ref_shrink_kappa, deliberately not a second magic number.
### `network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_elev_norm`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (if_phase_td_ref_elev_norm, false)  — **regime:** SS6.14.6 item 2.  The EWMA stores disp*<sin el> and the bar is rebuilt as k*hypot(s_z/sin(el_ref), 1.2533*s_raw/sqrt(n)) -- the sigma of the MEDIAN under test, rather than k x the family's raw scatter, which is a different quantity and blind to both el_ref and n.  DO NOT ARM YET.  Measured net COST on the sim bed and its benefit is on the detection side, which a 0-slip bed cannot measure.  Blocked on the SS6.14.6.4 fault arm.
- **evidence:** Statistic validated before the code was written (SS6.14.2): s_z ~ 12.8 mm at el_ref ~ 62 deg gives sigma_ref 14.5 mm vs a measured station-local common of 14.83, sigma_fam 10.1, hypot 17.7 vs a measured sigma(median) of 17.70.  DIRECTION PRE-REGISTERED then confirmed: offline replay over 314318 settled groups put the normalized bar at p50 0.78x the raw one (p10 0.67, p90 1.00; only 2.5% get a HIGHER bar), i.e. MORE sensitive -- and the 2 h arm (runs/ifg_we vs runs/ifg_w) duly ADDED 2 fires, 0 -> 2, both in the burst window, on a bed with zero slips.  More correct, measurably more expensive, benefit unmeasured.  NOTE the 25x reference-handover lift is NOT evidence for this knob: it was measured with the gate OFF against the fixed bar, where no empirical scale exists, and Card R already absorbs it.
- **interlock:** Same loader interlock as ref_warmup_abstain.  The physical FLOOR stays RAW in both modes (slip-QC lesson 7: a slip is a slip at any elevation); only the empirical arm is normalized.  Known wart: the cold prior is a raw scale while the EWMA is zenith-normalized, so the shrinkage blend mixes units while cold -- direction is conservative (~1.4x the raw bar at typical geometry) and ref_warmup_abstain is meant to be armed alongside.
### `network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_elev_clamp_deg`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (if_phase_td_ref_elev_clamp_deg, 5.0)  — **regime:** SS6.14.6 item 2 sub-knob: elevation clamp for the 1/sin(el) re-inflation. Inert unless ref_elev_norm is armed.
- **evidence:** Set to 5.0 to match the pool arm's own clamp so a horizon arc cannot explode the re-inflation; not independently swept.

### `processing.exclude_eclipsing_satellites`
- **default:** true in the standard armed set (armed on every camp60wu config)  — **regime:** stays ARMED — excluding shadow-transit attitude is still right.  The verdict is a CAVEAT, not a flip.
- **evidence:** STABILITY target 3 (2026-09-01, SPEC_synthetic_minimum_network §18.1, incl. the same-day marking CORRECTION; instrument scripts/simnet_sat_clock_census.py; valve row in SPEC_prefit_census_observability §13).  The returning sat's clock↔C5Q-bias split RE-REALIZES at re-admission (measured steps 6.3–6.7 m on G25/G12/G26, +1.81 on G22 mid-settled) and is held stiff for the rest of the run.  On THIS posture the edge is MARKED (Q3 `cta_disc_landed` lands the pending disc at return — R record, all per-sat counters, URA edge spike ~0.3 m decaying in 4–5 epochs, consumer resets) but NOT re-validated: the re-realization is signalled, never prevented, and the CLK-R magnitude tier is gap-blind (0 CLK_STEP on the same steps) so non-Q3 postures have no catcher at all.  The relocated ~7 m is a COLD-START C5Q third-frequency misrealization that was consumer-inert where it sat; datum posture decides who pays (r2q3/bca: the sat's clock, incl. the pivot G01 itself under bca; s0: smeared constellation-wide, bill moves to cb).
- **interlock:** Derived cards (not built): gap-aware clock-disc marking at re-admission; re-coupling split re-validation against the PRE-GAP realization; the C5Q cold-start misrealization itself (LV-6 family — keeping the 7 m parked in the consumer-inert C5Q channel may be the correct product decision).  **CONSUMER SIDE MEASURED on the 24 h bed (STABILITY target 1 verdict, §18.3): the valve's edges are the settled-stability driver** — edge-aligned consumer bias steps p50 0.060 m for ordinary sats but 0.31–0.83 m when the SATELLITE clock pivots G01/E02 (lowest-PRN elected, both eclipsing daily) transit; carry aging falsified on the same bed (best hours at 14–15 h age, DD error improves with chord age over 7.85M rows).  The biggest derived lever is satellite-pivot election avoiding eclipse-season sats / pivot-handover hygiene at the exclusion edge.

KEEP 0 in every frame-restore arm.  ⚠ UPDATE 2026-09-03 (§18.12): now LOADER-REFUSED alongside `network.resume_coupling_restore` — under conditional coupling `var` IS the row's pure conditional variance, so inflating it loosens the very row the regressors make border-neutral while the row still asserts Σ M·ŝ_prev. - **default:** false (src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:5089-5090); election at NetworkSbasis.cpp:252-275 (declared pin > sticky incumbent > lexical front)  — **regime:** RECOMMEND_SCOPED (2026-09-01; field-transfer measured 2026-09-02, SPEC §18.8): postures with MATERIAL per-sat re-realizations at re-admission (triple-frequency / weak-third-signal lineages — the sim bed family) — there it is THE E3 lever (settled consumer −74%). ### `network.resume_coupling_restore`  (§18.12 Card-1 coupling-regressor restore — read side)
- **default:** false (src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp `loadNetworkCarries_`, parsed after `resume_slip_warmup_ep`; restore block src/core/app/NetworkRunContext.cpp `networkTryWarmResume`, immediately AFTER the `resume_frame_restore` block and outside it)  — **regime:** BUILT + **MECHANISM VALIDATED** (SPEC §18.12, 2026-09-03) — **KEEP OFF pending the G20/E31 residual.**  NOT falsified: the emitted product improves ~10x.
- **evidence:** Three arms off ONE v3 snapshot (`runs/cplreg_q12warm_{a,b,c}`, sticky base), graded against the CONTINUOUS run.  Wire at the seam: clock between-sat SD **0.262 → 0.025 m (G)** and **0.144 → 0.030 (E)** vs frame-restore-only; phase L1C SD 0.037/0.064 → 0.029/0.019; code-side |dC| p50 0.638 → 0.062 m; satellites with metre-class dC **12+ → 2**; first-fold clock trips **31 → 2** (`nis` reason).  Removes three of the four §18.11 clock tails (G14 +1.21, G17 +1.17, G22 +1.37, G01 −0.89 all → small) and worsens the fourth (**G20 −1.34 → −2.25**, **E31 −0.89 → −1.12**).  Consumer FINAL 3D is the BEST of the three arms (0.108 vs 0.111 / 0.123).  Telemetry: 2811 L + 60 Z rows from 579,489 terms over a 990-column dictionary, **0 orphans**, **99.3 % of terms present** at the first fold (so NOT the rung-3 softening route), B1b PB σ 0.008 m.
- **why still OFF:** pre-registered stop rule 1 CROSSES — consumer h12 rms **2.053 m** (A 0.769, B 0.672), decaying 1.340/0.975/0.684 through h15, p50/rms 0.96–1.02 (a coherent BIAS).  Cause is the two residual satellites, not the mechanism: a single-station user cannot absorb one satellite at −2.25 m, whereas arm B's error spreads over 12 satellites at ~±1 m and partly absorbs into the receiver clock.  ⚠ The 15 `gap`-reason trips are **byte-identical between arms B and C** (same epochs, same satellites), so they are a pre-existing bed property; G20/E31 are merely the FIRST TWO, ~64 min after the seam, each one epoch after an `anchor_handover` away from S28000SIM.  In arm B those two had already been re-seeded by a seam trip; in arm C the PRESERVED anchor is carried into the gap re-draw and lands worse.  Second-order interaction, not a seam defect.
- **interlock:** Loader HARD-REQUIRES `resume_frame_restore`, `carry_conditional_coupling` and `carry_coupling_gauge_project` (Card 1f), and REFUSES `amb_seam_reinflate_var_m2 > 0` (row 321 — the rung-3 regression guard; tests/test_resume_coupling_restore.cpp `RestoreRefusesSeamReinflate`).  Needs a snapshot written under `state_snapshot_coupling_capture` (format **v3**); a v1/v2 file logs INERT with `n_resume_cpl_rows` 0 under an armed knob (invariant #4).  NOT in the warm-resume fingerprint (forward-only resume response policy, TWELFTH instance) so a same-snapshot OFF/ON A/B is legal — `35860c5f45b8a004` is unchanged.
- **next:** (1) the G20/E31 preserved-anchor gap re-draw — refresh the restored anchor's staleness basis, or re-tap at the `anchor_handover` edge (invariant #7: a handover is an EVENT and the staleness test fires one epoch later with both edges silent); (2) serialize `prev_carry_cov` — 376 border keys still fold DIAGONALLY at the first warm epoch; (3) census `rel p90` 1.05e-2 vs a pre-registered <1e-3 (p50 5.28e-4 is fine).

- **VERDICT 2026-09-06 (leg E, `runs/cplreg_q12warm_e`): RECOMMEND_SCOPED for warm-resume lineages** — with the dense border restored (QK/QP), `n_resume_cpl_diag_border` 376 → 0, first-fold nis trips 1, consumer h12 0.62 m rms / p50 0.10 (arm C 2.05 / 1.97; cold consumer on the continuous wire 0.52 / 0.12), h13 0.064; a COLD consumer replayed on the warm wire converges like on the continuous wire (SPEC_synthetic §18.14).  The arm-C bias was the one-epoch DIAGONAL border fold at the seam, not the residual satellites.  Posture row (regime warm); writers arm `state_snapshot_frame_capture` + `state_snapshot_coupling_capture`.

### `network.resume_carry_rebase`
- **default:** false at src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (jval "network.resume_carry_rebase", beside resume_frame_restore / resume_coupling_restore); driver Config::carry_rebase, CarriedBlock::unbased, the re-base in buildDdPriors (src/core/filter/NetworkInvariantCarryDriver.cpp); run-end line 'Network (RS-2 linearization carry)' in NetworkTeardown.cpp; the 'Linearization frame' token on the RTB-7c restore line; merged main 2c62724.  — **regime:** `warm and on("network.state_snapshot_carry")`.  **RECOMMEND_SCOPED.**  READ half of the RS-2 linearization-frame carry (SPEC_event_harness s21.4): at the FIRST DD prior build after a warm resume that restored ZL-bearing blocks, every carried chord the build projects is re-based onto THIS run's linearization, z_hat += W*zl_writer - zl_now, with W the cycle-basis projection the build already forms (frame-blind, so this is the general form of the per-chord rule and reduces to it when the tree is unchanged); Pz untouched; applied to the emitted block so a RAIM rebuild is idempotent.  A restored contributor with no ZL row cannot be re-based and its chords take the ordinary birth path (counted n_resume_carry_dropped_unbased).  Armed against a blob with no ZL rows: one loud warning and today's behaviour.  Forward-only resume response policy, NOT in the warm-resume fingerprint (same exemption class as resume_frame_restore).
- **evidence:** RS-2 (SPEC_event_harness s21.5, 2026-09-12): Phase 1 on the existing seed established the seam (control 60/60 withheld with dd_fold 7.8e7 and 676 D5 flags at +1; the no-blob leg 60/60 emitted with a00 within 2.25 TECU but a cold branch that re-realizes the phase wire - one declared R record naming 30 satellites, per-satellite-median-removed |dPB| 9.4 m, two no-blob legs disagreeing by 8.4 m, clock contrast growing 0.89 -> 3.41 m over 60 epochs; blob + SH gate off is garbage to +60, so the withhold era is the carried memory's).  P2 on runs/evseed_field6h_v2 ep0604 (SPEC_event_harness s21.6, 2026-09-12): single-knob resume_carry_rebase turns the field golden's warm resume from 0/61 withheld (a00 -508.96 TECU at +60, dd_fold 7.8e7, 676 D5 flags) into 61/61 emitted (a00 -0.61 TECU at +60, clock_std 0.90x, sigma0 1.13x); re-based 637 of 701 chords, the other 64 = arc churn at the seam, 0 un-basable; max |dz| 411715 m (the code-seeded linearization is hundreds of km from the writer's).  NOT YET SUFFICIENT: 301 restored chords are flagged by D5 at +1 (|v| p50 11.0 / p90 27 m against a modelled 0.58 m; L2W 171, L1C 113, L1W 17), 305 are reborn at +2, the emitter still re-realizes 30 satellites at +1 (one R record, as on the no-blob leg), and pooled +5..+60 the wire sits further from the seed than the no-blob leg's (clock contrast p50 3.64 vs 2.50 m; per-sat-median-removed |dPB| 13.8 vs 9.7 m; consumer 5.18 vs 5.05 m).  The interim harness posture for field legs stays state_snapshot_carry:false; the next increment (RS-2b, s21.6: D5 diagnostic-only for K post-resume epochs, network.resume_d5_warmup_ep) tests whether the restored branch is right and the cold station layer wrong, or the reverse.

### `network.resume_d5_warmup_ep`
- **default:** 0 at src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (jval "network.resume_d5_warmup_ep", beside resume_slip_warmup_ep; validated >= 0); countdown set in networkTryWarmResume, burned at the install seam (src/core/filter/NetworkInstallCarry.cpp); driver Config::setInnovDiagnosticOnly (src/core/filter/NetworkInvariantCarryDriver.cpp); merged main b6afe8c (worker cb1068d + c96182a).  — **regime:** `warm and on("network.state_snapshot_carry")`.  **RECOMMEND_SCOPED.**  Warm-resume LEGS that restore the invariant DD carry (state_snapshot_carry + resume_carry_rebase).  For the first K epochs after a WARM resume the D5 innovation gate is DIAGNOSTIC-ONLY: trips are counted (Counters::d5_warmup_suppressed, warmup_suppressed column on network_d5_flags.csv) but neither re-tapped nor funnelled into the next epoch's slip set; the sigma0 feed, the corroboration gate and the MW / LLI / IF-TD drains are untouched, so a real slip still refloats.  Armed only at a warm resume (a cold start never sets the countdown), counted down at the install seam, retire logged as the decoupling edge.  Forward-only resume response policy, NOT fingerprinted.  Why: the resumed run's first solve is made with a cold station layer, so the single-epoch data-only DD disagrees with the (correctly re-based) restored branch by |v| p50 11 m on 301 of 701 chords for exactly ONE epoch; acting on those trips erases the branch (305 rebirths at +2).  Validated value: 1.
- **evidence:** RS-2b (SPEC_event_harness s21.7, 2026-09-12): legs rs2_null_rebase_d5w{5,1} on runs/evseed_field6h_v2 ep0604 (blob + resume_carry_rebase; same exe): the 301 D5 trips at +1 are counted but not acted on, the +2 birth wave collapses 305 -> 7 in both windows, live D5 trips after the window are 0-2 per epoch (the seed's own rate: K=1 re-taps 5 chords over +2..+4, K=5 one at +7), the leg-wide D5 row count is 332 in every arm, availability / frame / branch-cost gates indistinguishable (a00 within 0.3 TECU of the base leg from +5, births ratio 0.86); the WIRE does not move (pooled clock contrast p50 3.59-3.61 vs 3.64 m, per-sat-median-removed |dPB| 14.35 vs 14.05 m, the R record at +1 still names 30 satellites), ZWD ~23 % quieter (pooled p50 2.14 -> 1.65 m), consumer K=1 5.24 vs base 5.18 m (K=5 8.91 - the in-process consumer is not a seam instrument, s18.14). ### `network.readmit_seed_horizon_s`  (READMIT continuity — E2 seed-first re-admission, BUILT 2026-09-05)
- **default:** 0.0 = OFF (include/config/GnssConfig.hpp `network_readmit_seed_horizon_s_`; parsed src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp beside `resume_coupling_restore`)  — **regime:** Q3-anchored lineages without `clock_model` — **VALIDATED ON THE WIRE (2026-09-05, `runs/readmit24h_e2`): T3 clock tails 2–4× smaller (G25 6.27 → 1.69 m, G12 5.38 → 2.71, G26 6.51 → 4.34), consumer post-eclipse excursion 2–3× smaller, DD unchanged; the code-bias half is wrong for third-frequency signals (see `readmit_seed_code_bias`); inert on the seam consumer (SPEC_synthetic §18.14)** — clock-only arm: FALSIFIED as an improvement (23:55 PDT).  26 clock seeds, 0 code-bias seeds; seed checks 20 held / 6 moved; 7 seed-attributed Q3 trips (arm 2: 8).  WIRE (settled-half per-sat clock): G12 6.26 m (arm 2 2.71, base 5.38), G26 6.15 (4.34, 6.51), G25 5.63 (1.69, 6.27) — the T3 tails RETURN.  CONSUMER h3–h8 1.76 / 0.71 / 0.66 / 0.78 / 0.34 / 0.13 m (arm 2 0.90 / 0.54 / 0.54 / 0.50 / 0.32 / 0.15).  DD unchanged (0.287 / 64.0 %, settled 0.430 / 53.0 %).  The IF-pair code-bias seeds anchor the clock-vs-code-bias SPLIT at the return, so the clock lands near the pre-gap realization even when its own seed is rejected; without them the cold third-frequency misrealization relocates into the clock — §18.1's T3 mechanism.  The third-frequency seed rejections are the stash-side check working, not harm.  `readmit_seed_code_bias` stays TRUE (default); `readmit_seed_horizon_s` 3900 s (both kinds) is the validated T3 fix → PROMOTED: ledger RECOMMEND_SCOPED (Q3-anchored lineages without clock_model, armed_when > 0), posture row, sim golden pin 3900 (manifest, regenerated golden, verify PASS, 20/20 smoke)..
- **mechanism:** a departed satellite's D2 clock + code-bias carry is PARKED (`CarryPrior::t_park_s`) for up to this many seconds — walked by every frame re-base that walks `prev_carry`, dropped at a clock-datum change of its constellation, never serialized — and on re-registration inside the horizon it SEEDS the column at `var + q·gap`: the Q3 anchor folds ANCHORED over its true per-satellite gap (bounded by this horizon instead of `clock_anchor_max_gap_s`) and its NIS guard is the mark-if-moved (a trip within 300 s of the seed is counted as the data rejecting it).  The phase-bias family is deliberately NOT seeded: its frame is re-realized at the incumbent-less cold re-pin (the leveling carries do not survive a 55 min absence at the 120 s leveling gap) and a stiff seed would fight the pin.
- **why:** the re-admission edge, measured three ways on the sim bed — T3's per-sat metre-class clock tails (§18.1: the return re-draws the clock LOOSE on a code-only border and the anchor's re-couple freezes it), the F3 block's ~4 h post-eclipse DD episodes (SPEC_amb_iono §6.9) and the G20/E31 residual of the §18.12 seam card (col_removed 305220 → col_added 306210 → `gap` trip 306240 → recouple 306330 on the re-draw) are ONE edge.
- **interlock:** NOT fingerprinted (parking adds entries and changes no carried value's meaning; a seam inside an absence loses its seed = today's cold return).  Under `clock_model` only code-bias columns can seed (loader warn).  Run-end `Network (READMIT continuity)` line prints BOTH edges (parked / seeded / expired / dropped / moved) — 0 seeds under parks > 0 reads as INERT-on-this-bed.
- **tests:** `ClockAnchorFold.SeededReturnFoldsAnchoredOverTheTrueGap`, `ReadmitContinuityConfig.*`.

### `network.readmit_coupling_marg_ep`  (READMIT re-coupling edge on the coupling rows / F3 block, BUILT 2026-09-05)
- **default:** 0 = OFF (include/config/GnssConfig.hpp `network_readmit_coupling_marg_ep_`; window opened at the stash tail of `networkStashCarry`, consumed by `CouplingRowBuilder::readmitDiverted`)  — **regime:** Card-1 coupling lineages (`carry_conditional_coupling`, not `carry_invariant`) — **KEEP OFF (2026-09-05, `runs/readmit24h_e2`): 28 windows / 2.98 M terms diverted and the DD identical to the base — inert on its target, no harm (SPEC_amb_iono §6.11 item 2)**.
- **mechanism:** for K epochs after a satellite's clock column returns from a >= 3-loop-epoch absence, every Card-1 coupling term on its shared columns takes the DEPARTED route (σ² += M²·P_prev, coefficient dropped) in every station's rows and F3 block — no carried ambiguity is conditioned on a column whose realization is still settling from its cold re-registration.  Both edges evented (`READMIT readmit` / `marg_expired`); the diverted-term count is the run-end denominator.
- **interlock:** INERT without `carry_conditional_coupling` (loader warn).  Not fingerprinted (the windows restart empty at a seam).
- **tests:** `NetworkCouplingTest.ReadmitWindowDivertsThePresentColumnAndExpires`.

- **⚠ CORRECTED 2026-09-06 (SPEC_amb_iono §6.12):** the target was an INSTRUMENT artifact — the DD trace's lexically-lowest reference satellite (G01/E02) returning from eclipse.  Ref-free (`scripts/simnet_dd_reref.py`) the base's DD is flat 0.07–0.11 cyc all day and this arm is identical to it in both frames.  KEEP OFF stands: no target.

### `network.phase_datum_handover_max_var_ratio`  (the honest-σ-scale PB-gauge handover ceiling, BUILT 2026-09-05)
- **default:** 0.0 = OFF (include/config/GnssConfig.hpp `network_phase_datum_handover_max_var_ratio_`; parsed beside `phase_datum_handover_max_var_m2`; tested in `networkElectPhaseAnchors`)  — **regime:** F1/F3 lineages under the continuous handover — **KEEP OFF (2026-09-05, `runs/ratio3h`): value 0.01 FALSIFIED — the one settled refusal was a converged successor whose first-stash variance was biased by earlier pin tenure, and refusing it put a 7.6 m PB step on the wire; the absolute 1e-2 stays the working value; denominator card owed (SPEC_amb_iono §6.11 item 3)**.
- **mechanism:** a ledger value is usable only if its conditional variance is <= ratio × the arc's FIRST-STASH conditional variance (`LocalCarry::var_first`: set at birth, kept across re-stashes, restarted by an erase-and-rebirth, kept by a re-level so a refloated arc reads unconverged until it re-earns the level); an unknown first-stash variance is refused (the 378-σ unvalidated-target guard stays armed).  The absolute ceiling stays as a rail.  Refusals are counted on `handover_refused_ratio` (disjoint from `handover_refused_var`) and every PBGAUGE handover row carries `var` / `var_first` when armed (the item-15 prerequisite).
- **why:** `phase_datum_handover_max_var_m2` is absolute and its lineage value (1e-4 m²) was sized to the over-counted σ of the diagonal leveling carry: under F1/F3's honest σ it refuses 93/93 successors and the 1e-2 that restores continuity is a hand value (SPEC_amb_iono §6.8.3).  The ratio asks the posture-invariant question — has THIS arc converged since its birth.
- **interlock:** INERT without `phase_datum_handover_continuous` (loader warn).  The L snapshot row gains an optional trailing `var_first` field ONLY when armed (default snapshots byte-identical); a pre-ratio file restores `var_first = 0`, so restored arcs are refused until they re-converge.
- **tests:** `PhaseDatumHandover.RatioCeilingRefusesUnconvergedAndUnknownArcs`, `PhaseDatumHandoverLoader.RatioCeilingDefaultsOffParsesAndRejectsNegative`.

### `network.phase_datum_candidacy_usable`  (COLD ANCHOR-FALLBACK: a usable carried value is a CANDIDACY condition, BUILT 2026-09-11)
- **default:** false = byte-identical shipped behaviour (include/config/GnssConfig.hpp network_phase_datum_candidacy_usable_{false} / network_phase_datum_candidacy_min_age_ep_{60}; parsed src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp beside phase_datum_handover_max_var_ratio, negative bar refused at load; resolved ctx.knobs.pd_candidacy_usable against pd_handover_continuous in NetworkRunContext.cpp; the pass itself in src/core/filter/NetworkPhaseDatum.cpp networkElectPhaseAnchors behind the AnchorCandidacyUsable view; counters on NetworkAnchorTallies; run-end clause in NetworkTeardown.cpp; trace fields cand_skipped / cand_first_unusable on the PBGAUGE and PHASE_ANCHOR handover rows; 17 tests tests/test_candidacy_usable.cpp).  — **regime:** prioritized-anchor lineages under the two-edge continuous handover — **KEEP_OFF.**  STORM ARM 2026-09-11 (runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_coldfb = cbscope5 + this knob, exe c763c168..., 1226 s, 720/720): the rule WORKS and the per-family SCOPE does not.  The candidacy pass moved 53 of 473 elections, settled cold handovers fell 51 -> 19 and are ZERO from hour 2 onward (the 27 that remain are the cold-start 30 min where every arc is young by construction), continuous successors are OLDER (arc-age p10 91 -> 99 ep), and every producer channel improved: ZWD settled truth rms 0.0069 -> 0.0061 and ref-free L1C 89.9 -> 90.9 (both the best of any arm on this bed), WL 99.2 -> 99.3, EWL 100.0, T4 11 = 11, W2 fallout absent, G4 keep-row census unchanged.  BUT the user's product is materially worse and worse than scope-OFF: consumer settled pooled p50 0.1204 -> 0.1794 m (+49 %, A 0.1741), window-excluded 0.1028 -> 0.1456, hours 1-3 roughly doubled (h5 and the last 36 min are the best of any arm).  The named cause is in the same run: split-referenced satellites 0.0 -> 0.9 mean / 3 max of 52.8, and the datum spread over +0.5 GPS / +0.2 Galileo anchor stations per epoch -- the screen is per-FAMILY, so a satellite's three bands can each elect their own best-carried host and the inter-frequency inconsistency is exactly what the per-band producer instruments cannot see (the shape SPEC_event_harness s16.7/s16.8 measured and the anchor clause cured there).  NEXT ITERATION, pre-registered: the SATELLITE clause -- families handed over in the same epoch elect ONE successor, the highest coverage-ranked station usable for the most of that satellite's bands; gates split-referenced back to 0.0/0, settled cold no worse than 19, consumer pooled p50 <= 0.1204.
- **mechanism:** COLD ANCHOR-FALLBACK (SPEC_event_harness §15 + §16.8).  The anchor election sweeps the coverage-ranked candidates and takes the FIRST that tracks the key; only AFTERWARDS does the continuous branch look that successor up in the one-epoch handover ledger and apply its usability predicate.  A family whose top-ranked tracker has no usable carried value therefore goes COLD — re-realizing the family's gauge, marking it for re-reference and erasing its leveling carries at every station — while a lower-ranked tracker holding a mature converged arc sits unused (cbscope5 storm arm: 43 of 47 non-cold-start cold handovers had >= 3 mature alternatives, and in NONE was the successor the best-placed candidate).  ON: where a handover is about to happen (an incumbent exists for the key and the sticky branch did not keep it) the sweep gains a FIRST pass over the SAME coverage-ranked list accepting only candidates whose carry is USABLE — the continuous branch's OWN predicate (one shared lambda, two readers), plus 'not itself freed this epoch' (both freed vehicles) and an arc-age bar (network.phase_datum_candidacy_min_age_ep, default 60 loop epochs; 0 = no test, unknown age = mature).  With none usable the election falls through to today's two sweeps unchanged, counted. PROPERTY: handover_cold then counts exactly the UNAVOIDABLE re-realizations.  Two deliberate non-entries: a kept sticky incumbent, and an incumbent-LESS key (a first sighting has no outgoing frame, the continuous branch cannot run for it, and every pin there is a 0-pin whichever station takes it).
- **interlock:** INERT without network.phase_datum_handover_continuous — with no ledger there is nothing to screen against, and the loader says so.  Election-side only: it changes WHICH station reaches the continuous branch, never a carried value's meaning, so a snapshot written OFF resumes ON and vice versa.  The hygiene eligibility test rides inside the pass, so the elected station is always one today's FIRST sweep would also have accepted — the pass can only move the choice LATER down the same coverage order.  A forced-cold injector directive is part of the shared predicate, so a harness cold leg cannot be silently re-pointed.  ⚠ network.phase_datum_candidacy_min_age_ep reads LocalCarry::birth_ep, which is NOT snapshotted and is re-stamped by the first post-resume harvest: on a WARM-RESUME lineage the bar refuses every candidate for its first min_age_ep epochs (the pass then falls through to today's election — the safe direction, but inert).  Set 0 to run on the ledger half alone, or take the owed card: snapshot birth_ep / birth_s, or give the bar a resume-aware clock.
- **evidence:** HARNESS (SPEC_event_harness §15; worker build cb1b427 on worktree coldfb off main cc689d5; quiet seed evseed_quiet6h ep0665, 60-epoch legs, event at +10, 16 legs).  G1 OFF byte-identity PASS twice: the unmodified-worktree exe 90566ebf... and the card exe c763c168... produce an IDENTICAL OFF null (products f3f37bc3..., network_dd_fractional 852c4dc3..., events 690927f6..., solution, zwd, and the run-end counter block); with the knob ARMED the solve is still byte-identical and only the two new trace fields appear on each handover row.  THE POPULATION IS NATURAL, NOT FORCED: all five injected anchor_handover families (E09, E10, G25, G10 = the §12.7 family, G30) hand over CONTINUOUSLY with the knob OFF — arc age and elevation at the snapshot epoch do NOT predict carry usability, so the §15 census's proxy is the wrong screen and the branch the election takes is the only oracle.  The cold population on this bed is the UNFORCED handover every leg carries at +22 (tow 280140): G30's sticky incumbent S25000SIM loses the key, the OFF election takes the top-ranked candidate S31000SIM at 12.59 deg whose ledger value the 0.01 m^2 ceiling refuses, and all three bands pin at 0 (COLD).  With the pass armed the same election skips exactly that candidate (cand_skipped=1, cand_first_unusable=S31000SIM in the trace) and elects S12000SIM at 39.81 deg, continuous at -13.65 / -11.86 / +3.97 m.  Six independent OFF/ON pairs (null, E09, E10, G25, G10, and the §12.7 40-cycle L1C slip at S29000SIM/G10 with anchor_slip_handover armed on both sides): handover_cold 3 -> 0 in EVERY pair; ZWD pooled truth rms 0.0721-0.0726 -> 0.0718 and max|e| 0.363 -> 0.335 m in every pair; consumer 3D better in five of six (-10 to -125 mm at the leg end) and WORSE by 110-128 mm on the slip leg, which is also the only leg where the pass moves more than the three G30 elections (9 rank changes) and the only one where the FREED clause fires (7 candidates refused because a slip response had freed their own arc that epoch).  T4 rows 0 in every leg both arms (quiet bed), so the T4 half of the gate is vacuous there.  ⚠ HARNESS LIMIT, measured: LocalCarry::birth_ep is not snapshotted AND the harvest re-stamps an unstamped entry at the current n_loop_epochs, so the first post-resume harvest re-brands every restored arc as born at the resume — on a warm-resume leg shorter than min_age_ep NO candidate can clear the bar and the pass is structurally inert (coldfb_null_on at the default 60: 24/24 elections fell through, 390 of 414 skips 'younger than 60 epochs', solve byte-identical to OFF). Every ON leg therefore runs min_age_ep 0, which exercises the LEDGER half of the predicate; the storm arm is a COLD 6 h run where the bar is live from epoch 60 on.  Tests: 17 cases in two new suites (tests/test_candidacy_usable.cpp); full Debug suite 1917 / 261 suites, 1900 passed, 17 skipped, 0 failures.
- **iteration 2 (the SATELLITE clause, `network.phase_datum_candidacy_satellite`, default TRUE under this knob):** ITERATION 2 — the SATELLITE clause (network.phase_datum_candidacy_satellite, default TRUE under the parent knob; OFF reproduces iteration 1 exactly, which is the single-knob A/B).  RULE: (a) every family of satellite s handing over this epoch takes ONE successor — the first coverage-ranked candidate usable for the LARGEST number of them, ties to coverage order; a family that host is not usable for is pinned THERE through the cold path (a frame step, not a split); (b) a PARTIAL handover prefers the station already anchoring the satellite's other families, taken on 'usable for >= 1' (a READING, not a transcription — the only one under which (c) holds); (c) the run-end clause reports the distinct-anchor census from the SAME counters the storm grade reads.  HARNESS: the nulls and the G30 leg are BYTE-IDENTICAL to their iteration-1 twins (those windows had no split to avoid, and sat_clause_split_avoided = 0 says so); the SLIP leg — the one place iteration 1 hurt — goes split-referenced 4.3/6 (OFF) -> 6.0/7 (iter 1) -> 4.3/6 (iter 2, exactly OFF) and consumer-at-leg-end 1.0614 (OFF) -> 1.1718 (iter 1, +110 mm) -> 1.0240 (iter 2, 37 mm BETTER than OFF) with handover_cold 3 -> 0 kept; the emitted G10 wire is the same in both iterations (clock d -0.0007 m, L1C -0.039, L2W -0.046), so the consumer gain is the satellites whose references iteration 1 split.  STORM ARM coldfb2 (720/720, 1406 s, exe 1b80ce25...): split-referenced 0.0 / max 0 ALL RUN (the primary gate, met exactly; 13 splits avoided over 133 satellite-epochs / 399 families, 0 cold co-located, 0 untracked), datum spread back to C5's (17.16 vs 17.26 GPS anchor stations/epoch), successor age p10 91 -> 116, cold handovers 46 total / 19 settled and ZERO from hour 2, PB-GAUGE cold 52, ZWD 0.0061, WL 99.3, L1C 90.8, T4 11, W2 absent, G4 unchanged — 11 of 14 gates PASS.  ⚠ THE THREE THAT FAIL ARE THE CONSUMER, AND THEY FALSIFY THE ITERATION-1 READING: pooled settled p50 0.1794 -> 0.1941 (bar <= 0.1204), window-excluded 0.1456 -> 0.1751 (bar <= 0.1028), hourly h1-h3 +9 to +15 cm against the +2 cm allowance.  Every split removed and the consumer 1.5 cm WORSE: the split reference was a CORRELATE of the consumer cost, not its mechanism.  What the C5 comparison is actually measuring is a single-realization horizontal datum bias — settled mean EAST error is +7.7 / +7.8 / +7.9 cm on the scope-OFF baseline, iteration 1 and iteration 2 alike and +3.8 cm on cbscope5, so C5 is the outlier; the static bias-vector magnitude is 6.3 cm (C5) vs 9.9 cm (iter 2), ~3.6 cm of the 7.4 cm median gap, and against scope-OFF the card costs +0.5 cm (iter 1) / +2.0 cm (iter 2), not +49 %.  Standing invariant #5 names exactly this about consumer position as a grading channel.  ITERATION 3, pre-registered: (i) a DATUM-INVARIANT consumer instrument (between-satellite emitted-bias consistency, or the consumer's fixing / residual channel) and a re-grade of every arm on it — until that exists no consumer gate on this bed distinguishes a card from a realization; (ii) the COVERAGE cost of a usable-carry host — the pass elects a later-ranked station by construction (60 rank changes in 6 h), so measure the elected successors' rank distribution and test a restraint that accepts a later-ranked candidate only while its coverage is within k of the top one's (k a knob, 0 = today), gated on keeping iteration 2's producer channels with the consumer inside the OFF arm's own spread.
- **iteration 3 (the datum-invariant consumer census, no runs):** ITERATION 3 — the DATUM-INVARIANT consumer census (SPEC_event_harness §15.10; scripts/ev_consumer_invariant_grade.py over the four existing storm arms, NO new runs).  THE RAW POOLED MEDIAN INVERTS THE RANKING against the scope-OFF arm: raw it reads A 0.1741 < B 0.1794 < B2 0.1941 (the card 'costs' the user), and with each arm's own PER-HOUR mean ENU removed it reads B2 0.0815 < B 0.0943 < A 0.1532 — the card is 47 % BETTER than OFF and iteration 2 better than iteration 1.  The difference is A's −16 cm vertical bias, which the raw median charges to nobody.  BUT cbscope5's advantage SURVIVES de-biasing (debias p50 0.0646, per-hour 0.0521, rms 0.206–0.212 — best of the four), so the §15.8 gate failures are NOT an artifact of C5's centring and B2 is better than A / worse than C5 on the position channel both before and after de-biasing.  THE INSTRUMENTS THAT MOVE WITH THE COLD-HANDOVER COUNT ARE THE WIRE ONES, and on those B2 beats BOTH A and C5: the consumer's own SSR phase-bias RESETS are monotone in the cold count (78 colds -> 1938 resets on C5, 68 -> 1867 on A, 46 -> 1772 / 1794 on B / B2), and the emitted-bias step instrument separates card from no-card sharply (fraction of epochs carrying any |step| > 5 cm 0.371 / 0.369 -> 0.306 / 0.308; p90 of the per-epoch maximum step 0.528 / 0.400 m -> 0.268 / 0.270 m).  NEGATIVE CONTROLS that do NOT move: code-bias discontinuities (599 / 597 / 593 / 593) and clock discontinuities (40 / 39 / 39 / 39) are flat, and SHOCKS (> 0.5 m after h1) are ANTI-correlated (C5 13 with the most colds, B2 26, A 46) and live in h0 and h4–h5 — ignition and the §19 pivot-departure window, not the handover population.  NOT COMPUTABLE on these arms: the AR channel and the wrong-fix rate — closed_loop.ar_mode = 0 on this lineage, so ar_ran is 0 on all 720 epochs of all four arms, ar_gate reads not_run everywhere, p_succ is 0 and ambiguity_resolver.log is empty; the consumer is float-only by configuration and grading fixes needs a new arm, not a new instrument.  VERDICT UNCHANGED: the promotion condition (a datum-invariant channel where B2 beats BOTH A and C5, AND no invariant channel worse) is half met — the wire instruments and the PB-reset count qualify, the de-biased position p50/rms and the shock count are worse than C5 — so KEEP_OFF stands.  What iteration 3 changes is the question: not 'does the card cost the user 49 %' (it does not; that was a vertical bias on the OFF arm) but 'the card makes the WIRE quieter and the position channel still prefers cbscope5', and the next place to look is the §19 pivot-departure window, where every arm's shocks and half its PB resets live and the cold-handover population does not.
- **iteration 4 (re-graded on the NEW golden, after the scoped_handover_response promotion):** ITERATION 4 — the card re-graded on the NEW golden (SPEC_event_harness §15.11; runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_coldfb3 = the tracked _scoped arm + this knob and the run path only, 720/720, 1361 s, exe 1B37E94B from main e90086f; reference S = runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_scoped on F4FF8EF0, which differs only in the LOG-ONLY posture table and in this default-OFF card, certified byte-identical OFF at §15.3).  The scoped_handover_response promotion moved the baseline — S's consumer is settled p50 0.0825 with ZERO shocks — so this re-asks §15.9's question against it, on §15.10's invariant instruments.  12 OF 15 GATES PASS.  On the WIRE the card now beats the best arm this bed has, on every instrument at once: consumer PB resets 1475 -> 1337 (-9.4 %), fraction of epochs carrying an emitted |step| > 5 cm 0.3611 -> 0.2944 (-18 %), p90 per-epoch max step 0.400 -> 0.265 m (-34 %), with settled cold handovers 51 -> 19 and ZERO from hour 2, successor arc-age p10 91 -> 116, split 0.0/0 kept and 13 splits avoided by the satellite clause.  Producer truth channels all better: ZWD 0.0066 -> 0.0060 and ref-free L1C 90.2 -> 91.0 (both the best on this bed), WL 99.2 -> 99.3, T4 11 = 11, W2 absent, sat-clock datum-free contrast +0.73 % (bar +-3 %), CB/clock discs flat (517/21 -> 516/21), shocks 0 = 0, GPS pivot edge 0.126 -> 0.104 m.  THE THREE FAILURES ARE TWO INSTRUMENTS: the per-hour-DE-BIASED consumer (p50 0.0347 -> 0.0387, rms 0.0466 -> 0.0516 — a 4-5 mm miss, not a collapse) and the GALILEO pivot edge (321900: 0.031 -> 0.108 m, still 7x better than the pre-scoped 0.777 but a step back from S).  The settled mean EAST error returns to +0.083 m — the value every candidacy arm and the scope-OFF arm carry — from S's +0.034, so a third of the raw gap is again a static horizontal realization; the DE-BIASED gap nonetheless survives, exactly as cbscope5's did in §15.10.  VERDICT UNCHANGED: KEEP_OFF.  The open question is now narrow — a quieter wire with a noisier consumer, and an untested interaction at a pivot edge where the candidacy pass re-chooses hosts in the same epoch a clock datum moves.  ITERATION 5 pre-registered: (i) a per-edge anchor-set census across 321900 in S and B3 (which families moved, which moved BECAUSE of the pass, whether the consumer's resets there are the ones the card added); (ii) only if the census names one, an interlock that defers a candidacy-driven handover by one epoch when the same epoch carries a clock-datum change, gated on the Galileo edge returning to <= 0.031 m with the wire instruments kept.
- **tests:** `CandidacyUsableConfig.*` (5), `CandidacyUsableElection.*` (19) in `tests/test_candidacy_usable.cpp` — 24 cases over both iterations, every satellite-clause case asserting the per-satellite distinct-station count.

### `network.phase_leveling_local_block_min_age_ep`  (F3 block ADMISSION age gate, BUILT 2026-09-05)
- **default:** 0 = OFF (include/config/GnssConfig.hpp `network_phase_leveling_local_block_min_age_ep_`; parsed beside `phase_leveling_local_block`; split in ApplicationNetworkAssembly.cpp; `LocalCarry::birth_ep` kept at the leveling stash)  — **regime:** F3 lineages — arm 5 (young legs as DIAGONAL rows) FAIL — every hour back at the pre-F1 over-count, any diagonal leveling row re-asserts the station-common directions (§6.11 item 6); `young_mode` 'free' (default) built; arm 5b: FAIL — the newborn-leg hypothesis is falsified too (23:05 PDT).  Hourly L1C (5b / base): h2 0.221 / 0.192, h3 0.214 / 0.198, h4 0.308 / 0.232, h5 0.350 / 0.271, h6 0.554 / 0.424, h7 0.588 / 0.485; WL 84–92 % (base 96–99 %); clean chords h6 0.761 / h7 0.616 (base 0.538 / 0.478) — the episode LARGER, not smaller; mature legs h2–h3 0.175 / 0.169 (base 0.160 / 0.167); free young legs 1.7–2.0 cyc (base carried young 0.74 / 0.64, the additional falsifier hit); 232,089 young columns kept out of 55,440 blocks.  Removing the newborn legs from the block does not protect the mature chords through the cluster.  With arm 2, the post-eclipse DD episodes are NEITHER the returned satellite's shared columns NOR the newborn legs' block membership; both age-gate responses fail → `phase_leveling_local_block_min_age_ep` KEEP OFF (free = harmless but useless, diagonal = harmful).  The base's event census through the h16–h21 episode shows no datum / era / withhold events (anchor handovers 12–33 and ~20 T1 refsat_resets per 20 min, 7-column col_added/removed per return) — the next card is a fresh investigation of what re-realizes NETWORK-WIDE at a re-admission cluster that the exactly-conditioned F3 block resists (prefit census / topology ledger / rhs_attrib), not another gate..
- **mechanism:** a carried leg younger than K loop epochs since its first stash is kept OUT of its station's jointly-whitened block and injected as a per-column row (F1-scaled over the young set when `phase_leveling_los_scale` is armed); once mature it joins the block.  Counted per station-epoch (`pl_blk_young_cols`, on the run-end F3 line).
- **why:** the ~4 h network-wide DD episodes after each eclipse re-admission cluster (§6.9) are NOT the shared border — `runs/readmit24h_e2` seeded the returned satellite's clock and marginalized its columns out of every block for 20 epochs (2.98 M terms) and the settled-quarter DD did not move by a digit (0.420 vs 0.425 cyc).  By elimination the legs BORN at the re-admission (every station sees the returning satellite at once) carry their code-level birth error into the block, and the joint whitening spreads it into the mature columns at their tight variance (§3.1's birth-frame inheritance, F3-amplified — the reason F1's diagonal control showed a smaller bump).
- **interlock:** INERT without the block (loader warn).  Not fingerprinted; `birth_ep` is not snapshotted (a restored entry reads mature).

- **VERDICT 2026-09-06: KEEP OFF** — both responses fail (diagonal harmful, free useless; SPEC_amb_iono §6.11 items 6–7).

- **⚠ CORRECTED 2026-09-06 (SPEC_amb_iono §6.12):** the target episode was the DD trace's reference satellite returning from eclipse.  Re-graded ref-free: arm 5 (diagonal) 1.3–2.7 cyc every hour — REAL harm in every frame; arm 5b (free) 0.08–0.12 vs base 0.07–0.09 cyc — a small cost, no benefit.  KEEP OFF stands on corrected grounds.

### `network.readmit_seed_code_bias`  (readmit seed scope sub-knob, BUILT 2026-09-05)
- **default:** true (include/config/GnssConfig.hpp `network_readmit_seed_code_bias_`; parking filter in `networkStashCarry`)  — **regime:** with `readmit_seed_horizon_s` — arm `runs/readmit24h_e2clk`: FALSIFIED as an improvement (23:55 PDT).  26 clock seeds, 0 code-bias seeds; seed checks 20 held / 6 moved; 7 seed-attributed Q3 trips (arm 2: 8).  WIRE (settled-half per-sat clock): G12 6.26 m (arm 2 2.71, base 5.38), G26 6.15 (4.34, 6.51), G25 5.63 (1.69, 6.27) — the T3 tails RETURN.  CONSUMER h3–h8 1.76 / 0.71 / 0.66 / 0.78 / 0.34 / 0.13 m (arm 2 0.90 / 0.54 / 0.54 / 0.50 / 0.32 / 0.15).  DD unchanged (0.287 / 64.0 %, settled 0.430 / 53.0 %).  The IF-pair code-bias seeds anchor the clock-vs-code-bias SPLIT at the return, so the clock lands near the pre-gap realization even when its own seed is rejected; without them the cold third-frequency misrealization relocates into the clock — §18.1's T3 mechanism.  The third-frequency seed rejections are the stash-side check working, not harm.  `readmit_seed_code_bias` stays TRUE (default); `readmit_seed_horizon_s` 3900 s (both kinds) is the validated T3 fix → PROMOTED: ledger RECOMMEND_SCOPED (Q3-anchored lineages without clock_model, armed_when > 0), posture row, sim golden pin 3900 (manifest, regenerated golden, verify PASS, 20/20 smoke)..
- **why:** on `runs/readmit24h_e2` the IF-pair code-bias seeds held 44/44 while the THIRD-frequency seeds (C5Q, C7Q) were rejected by the data 28/40 (|e|/√S ≫ 6, up to 42 cm) and 5 of 26 anchored clock seeds were rejected in-epoch on the T3 satellites (e 0.18–0.37 m) — the §18.1 cold-start third-frequency misrealization carried into the return through a stiff (σ 1.9 cm) seed.  false = the clock alone is parked and seeded; the code biases re-solve from data as today.

- **VERDICT 2026-09-06: RECOMMEND_SCOPED (default true; the clock-only arm is falsified — the T3 tails return without the code-bias seeds).**  Posture row (regime `readmit_seed_horizon_s > 0`).

### `network.state_snapshot_coupling_capture`  (§18.12 coupling-regressor capture — write side)
- **default:** false (include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp NetworkConfig, beside `state_snapshot_frame_capture`)  — **regime:** SAFE to arm on any writer whose snapshots may be resumed with `resume_coupling_restore`; OFF elsewhere so default snapshots stay v1/v2.
- **evidence:** Write-side only — the capture block reads `ctx.carry` AFTER the emit and writes only into `snap`.  Writer gate PASSED: `runs/camp60wu_quiet12h_frwriter_sticky/products.txt` byte-identical to `runs/camp60wu_quiet24h_sticky/products.txt` over all **34,840,533 bytes**.  Its `.ep1440` carries **2811 QL = 2811 L** and **60 QZ = 60 Z** (exact 1:1), 990 QS, `END` guard exact, 0 rows dropped.
- **⚠ SIZE — the card's own estimate was wrong by ~40x.**  Handoff §3 budgeted "~2811 rows x few entries = 250 KB".  Measured: **130.9 terms/row** run-average (526,296,304 + 84,799 terms over 4,020,945 stashes) and **201.5** at the settled branch epoch, so `.ep1440` is **15.2 MB** and a 12 h leg with `state_snapshot_keep_history` wrote **15 GB** before pruning to the branch file.  Plan disk BEFORE launching.  Do NOT close the gap by sparsifying harder or truncating coefficient precision — that is the Card-1e trip regime.
- **interlock:** Loader HARD-REQUIRES `state_snapshot_frame_capture` (a coupling-only snapshot is unusable: the restore is only reachable behind `resume_frame_restore`, which is INERT without frame rows).  INERT without `state_snapshot_path`.  Puts the file under the format-**v3** header, which a pre-card binary CANNOT parse (strict tag switch) and which ABORTS under `state_snapshot_require` — a binary-vintage question, not a fingerprint one.  Not fingerprinted.

### `network.resume_frame_restore`  (§18.10 resume FRAME restore — read side)
- **default:** false (src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp, parsed beside amb_seam_reinflate_var_m2; restore block NetworkRunContext.cpp networkTryWarmResume after the ZWD-seam arming; election consumer NetworkPhaseDatum.cpp:302-308 sticky path + :355-361 R2 clause)  — **regime:** BUILT + MECHANISM VERIFIED (SPEC §18.11, 2026-09-03) — **KEEP OFF pending the G20/E31 residual.**  ⚠ UPDATE 2026-09-03 (§18.12): the coupling-regressor increment IS BUILT (`network.resume_coupling_restore`) and VALIDATED on the wire — it takes the first-fold trips 31 → 2 and the metre-class satellite count 12+ → 2.  Arm this row TOGETHER with the coupling restore, never alone.  On the r2q3 sim bed the seam closes on the phase side (PB σ 0.008 m at the first warm epoch vs 1.112; no datum mark; 54 carries erased vs 2862; consumer resets 5 vs 51; PB SD vs the continuous run 0.04–0.08 m vs 8–12 m) but 33 of the 52 restored clock anchors TRIP at the first fold (w 6–25) because the restored leveling rows carry no Card-1 coupling regressors and out-vote the anchored clock priors; the LOOSE re-draw freezes new metre-class clock tails on G14/G17/G20/G22 (+1.0–1.4 m over the continuous run) and the consumer pays at h19 (0.96 rms) more than it saved at h13–h15 (0.17/0.14/0.11 vs 0.25/0.34/0.30).  Rung 3 (+ amb_seam_reinflate_var_m2 0.0025) FALSIFIED the variance-inflation proxy: trips 33 → 14 but a 5.1 m consumer walk in h12 (2.9 / 1.8 / 1.1 m in h13–h15) — the restored levels re-floated on the cold station layer.  The fix is the coupling regressors, not the stiffness.
- **evidence:** SPEC_synthetic_minimum_network §18.9 (mechanism, five-reader map) + §18.11 (verdict).  What it restores at a warm resume: the per-(sat,signal) phase-anchor incumbents + R2 sticky pin targets (without them the first election runs incumbent-less, the QUEUE-1(d) clause marks all 162 families, erases all 2811 restored leveling carries — run-end "erased" 2862 vs 129 — and marginalizes their D2 phase-bias prior: PB σ 1.112 m vs 0.012 at the first warm epoch), the Q3 clock-anchor per-sat state with t_last (without it every clock folds LOOSE at σ 0.55 m for the re-couple horizon and re-engages fleet-wide on the re-split: 48+4 cta_recouple at 302520/302550 vs 0), the CLK-R held frame (without it the emitted common mode restarts at 0 and the F1 `resume_seam` datum mark fires), and it primes the handover ledger from the restored leveling rows (Card-3 graft) so a family whose incumbent no longer tracks takes the continuous-handover path.  Same-snapshot OFF control reproduces leg A to the digit (structural control PASS); a fresh consumer replayed from h18 on the ON wire reproduces the h19 excursion (1.14–1.19) and not on the continuous wire (0.25–0.33) — the residual is a wire property of the tripped clocks.
- **interlock:** Needs a snapshot written under `network.state_snapshot_frame_capture` (format v2); a v1 snapshot restores empty maps and the run logs INERT (invariant #4: `n_resume_frame_anchors` 0 under an armed knob).  NOT in the warm-resume fingerprint (forward-only resume response policy, NINTH instance — NetworkRunContext.cpp exemption block) so a same-snapshot OFF/ON A/B is legal.  ⚠ `amb_seam_reinflate_var_m2` (row 321) becomes ACTIVE once the erase stops — keep it 0 unless re-validated.  The constellation-level `CLK_FRAME resume_seam` mark is suppressed whenever the engine frame is restored; a family the election still cold-pins is marked individually by 1(d) and by the emitter's 0.05 m jump test (accepted identity gap, §18.10).  Requires `phase_datum_prioritized` sticky election and Q3 (`clock_temporal_anchor`) to have anything to restore; harmless otherwise.

- **VERDICT 2026-09-06: RECOMMEND_SCOPED for warm-resume lineages** (paired with `resume_coupling_restore` on a QK/QP snapshot — leg E, SPEC_synthetic §18.14: the warm restart's consumer converges like a cold consumer on the continuous wire).  Posture row (regime warm).

### `network.resume_slip_warmup_ep`  (§18.10 post-resume MW/LLI drain warm-up)
- **default:** 0 = OFF (GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp beside resume_frame_restore; countdown NetworkRunContext.hpp `resume_slip_warmup_left`, dispatch ApplicationNetworkAssembly.cpp ~:1500)  — **regime:** Validated on the ON leg as a SAFE response (146 MW detections at the second warm epoch logged "detected (carry cold; diagnostic)", 0 acted; RETIRED at epoch 5; the T1 refsat reset at the same epoch still acted).  Arm together with resume_frame_restore; pointless alone (the cold-pin erase makes the burst moot).

For K processed epochs after a resume the T0 (MW/LLI) drain's carry_warm is forced false at the single dispatch site; the T1 IF-TD and wind-up drains keep their live gate.  ON leg run-end: MW/LLI drain diagnostic-only for 5 post-resume epoch(s); slip re-level counters 1638/1506 (leg A 2294/1824 — the ~290-latch seam excess is gone).
