### `network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_pairs`
- **default:** first = byte-identical shipped behaviour (main bbc8273, merged 2026-09-11; worker commits ace5d3e + 1ef8ea8 on worktree agent-a0887be88117c9ad4).  Loader src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (cycle_slip block; enum NetworkIfPhaseTdPairs First|All in include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp); detector include/slip/IfPhaseSlipDetector.hpp + src/core/slip/IfPhaseSlipDetector.cpp (per-pair chains <sys>|<sig1>_<sig2>, the first pair keeps the bare-system chain; per-pair resetSatelliteInState and reset keys); driver networkSlipIfTdDriver in src/core/slip/NetworkSlipQc.cpp; per-pair-kind tally and veto breakdown in NetworkTeardown.cpp ('Network (IF-TD pairs [all]): N pair kind(s), eval / flag / refloat'); the verdict (NetworkCommonBandScope) reads every evaluated pair's flag; 14 tests tests/test_if_td_pair_width.cpp.  — **regime:** `on("network.phase_leveling") and on("network.cycle_slip.mw_detect") and on("network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td")`.  **RECOMMEND_ON.**  first (default) = the IF-TD phase time-difference detector forms ONE pair per satellite, the first two distinct phase bands (L1C+L2W on GPS, L1C+L5Q on Galileo) -- today's byte-identical behaviour.  all = every distinct pair of the satellite's tracked phase bands (3 on a triple-band arc), each with its own pool (centre / scale / shrinkage), gradient chain, reset keys and per-pair-kind counters; additional chains adopt the first chain's reference satellite (s14.10.4) so the ref gate is not flooded by the 12-19x noise amplification of the close-spaced pairs; drains keep today's per-pair semantics (with the common-band verdict ON, pair bands intersected with implicated); T1_if_slip rows carry pair= under all only.  MECHANISM (SPEC_event_harness s14.10, s16): the common-band verdict vetoes a silent MW pair only when the IF-TD on THAT pair flagged; with one IF pair per satellite the same-epoch equal-count trap (two bands slipping by near-equal counts leave their MW lane silent) is structurally open on the other pairs -- the storm bed carried 9 near-equal adjacent slip pairs in 6 h, 6 on pairs the IF-TD never formed.
- **evidence:** FP-1 (SPEC_event_harness s20.2, 2026-09-13; the s20 matrix re-run on the warm-resuming field seed evseed_field6h_v5 ep0604, legs runs/evleg_fp1_*): G-A reproduces every pre-registered row (E1 {L5X}; E2 all = the clause's cost; E3/G24 all via ref_arc = the trap closed; E3/G25 all via the (1,5) veto) and the G25 control is clean, BUT if_phase_td_pairs=all fans a slip ON a chain's reference satellite out to every other satellite of that chain (G24 = both L5 chains' reference at GOPE: E1 six false refloats on G11/G25/G32 at 253-1580x the threshold, an innocent satellite's phase bias re-realized network-wide by 13.7 m, consumer mean |d3D| 4.71 vs 0.98 m; no backstop - card R tests the first chain only, T4 = 0, no scope row to narrow) => G-B/G-C/G-D FAIL on every G24 leg; NOT pinned.  Counter-weight: at +39 a real 46-sigma (1,5) slip at JPLM/G10 is caught by all and missed by first (pair-rotation pool restart).  The fix is REF-FAN (s22.6, network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_arm_all); the pin is re-run once it passes F1-F4.  FIELD HARNESS (SPEC_event_harness s20, 2026-09-12): FIELD EXCEPTION -- field legs at GOPE00CZE/G25 (equal-count L1C+L5X +29, seed ep0604): all == the (L1C,L5X) IF-TD deviation reads 3.1508 m against a 0.1089 m threshold (29x; 29 cyc x 0.107 m/cyc = 3.10 m predicted) -> veto -> all three bands freed exactly as OFF; first == the pair is never evaluated -> the scope keeps L2W (the innocent band).  The close-spaced pools are healthy on real data (L2W/L5X threshold 0.17 m; the 12x amplification is absorbed; 6 h seed: L1C_L5Q / L1C_L5X pairs 0 flags).  BUT the additional chains do not test their own reference satellite (deviation identically 0 on one arc per station = the highest L5-capable satellite; the first chain's reference arm is not re-run, s14.10.4), so the veto is dead exactly there (G24 at GOPE) - pinned TOGETHER with common_band_scope once the reference-arc clause lands and the field seed can warm-resume (Card 1f).  SPEC_event_harness s20.1.  HARNESS (SPEC_event_harness s14.10-s14.12, s16; worker build ace5d3e + 1ef8ea8 on worktree agent-a0887be88117c9ad4, merged bbc8273; manager re-grade): G1 first + scope OFF byte-identical to main 420634f on the quiet 240-epoch null (products 4c96e34a...) and the L2 control (2c58b97f...), every artifact incl. events, rx-states and the consumer trace; S2 (L1C -29 and L5Q -29 in one epoch at S21000SIM/G08, quiet seed ep0665): scope alone -> keep implicated=L2W (the wrong narrowing, iono step 3.8 m); with all pairs -> all, basis iftd(L1C+L5Q)=flagged, products digit-for-digit = OFF; L2 control (L2W -33): all == first byte-for-byte, still narrowed to L2W (level residual 0.0003 vs 0.33 cyc OFF); S1 (LLI-flagged L5Q -31 then L1C -29): closed by rule F2, not by the pairs (the (1,5) IF-TD had fired and reset on the LLI epoch) -> all / lli_shadow(n=10), residuals = OFF; S3 (LLI-flagged L5Q alone): under all WITHOUT rule F3 the pairs containing L5Q fired unscoped at the LLI epoch (level residual 0.005 -> 0.335 cyc at +45); with F3 all == first byte-for-byte on S1, S3, L2 and the 70-epoch null -- S2 is the only leg where the extra pair changes the solve.  Shared reference arm (s14.10.4): per-chain reference-slip tests took the quiet null's IF-TD ref gate from 25 candidates / 1 fire to 5,351 / 64 because (L2W,L5Q) amplifies noise 16.6x vs 2.98x on (L1C,L2W); additional chains adopt the first chain's reference and 25 / 1 is restored.  Tests 1897 / 259, 0 failures (+14 tests/test_if_td_pair_width.cpp, +9 common-band).  MW detector decision lines (MWDEC/MWSKIP, station-labelled) now exist behind the detector-log level: the detector WITHHOLDS the LLI epoch's sample for the whole satellite and keeps the arc, so the next sample reads the combined jump -- the mechanism of every wrong narrowing on the storm bed. COSTS, named: PHASE_ANCHOR cold handovers 68 (OFF) / 51 (cbscope4) -> 78 -- the anchor clause moves all three families of a satellite together at a flagged epoch on an anchor arc, and the successor's other-band arcs are not always carry-usable (cold re-realizations 73 -> 100); that one mechanism also holds ZWD at 0.0069 against cbscope4's 0.0064 and L1C at 89.9 against 90.1 (both better than OFF); the remedy is the cold anchor-fallback card (SPEC s15: candidacy requires a usable carried value on every band). - **interlock:** Detection-side knob: it changes which IF pairs are evaluated, never a carried value's meaning, so a snapshot written first resumes all and vice versa.  Inert without the IF-TD detector.  With the common-band verdict OFF, all changes a response only when every MW pair is silent and only a non-first IF pair flags (the all-bands-equal case), absent from the sim bed; with the verdict ON it is the veto's coverage and must be armed with network.cycle_slip.common_band_scope (whose rule F3 keeps the new pairs' drains scoped at receiver-flagged epochs -- without F3, all turned every LLI epoch into an all-band refloat, S3 level residual 0.005 -> 0.335 cyc).  The close-spaced pairs amplify phase noise 12x (L2W,L5Q) and 19x (L5Q,L7Q): their pools are their own, and a one-cycle equal slip on those two bands stays marginal (~1.5 sigma) -- a 25 cm misfit the outlier tier catches in one epoch; the realized trap (large slips, small difference) is metres in every pair.

### `network.cycle_slip.common_band_ref_arc`
- **default:** false at src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (jval "network.cycle_slip.common_band_ref_arc", beside common_band_min_arc_epochs; warn+disarm without common_band_scope); NetworkConfig cycle_slip_common_band_ref_arc; rule branch src/core/slip/NetworkCommonBandScope.cpp (the preview carries IfJumpInfo::is_reference, IfTdPreview); merged main 4b88ca2 (worker 6d3c304 + c8096ee).  — **regime:** `on("network.cycle_slip.common_band_scope")`.  **RECOMMEND_ON.**  The common-band scope's IF-TD veto can only fire where the pooled IF-TD test can flag, and on a chain's REFERENCE satellite it cannot: that sample's double difference is identically 0 by construction (IfJumpInfo::is_reference), so `|dd - med| > thr` is structurally false however large the slip and the pair lands in iftd_silent.  Armed, such a pair is UNTESTED for the verdict (CommonBandEvidence::iftd_untested) and is refused as an exonerator exactly as immature / lli_shadow are (token iftd(<pair>)=ref_arc, counter iftd_ref_arc with a per-pair breakdown, run-end 'ref-arc refusals'); the verdict falls back to today's all-band response when nothing else exonerates.  Applies to every chain's reference, first chain included (its per-station reference gate is a separate instrument whose decision the preview never sees).  No detector change; network_if_slip.csv gains an is_ref column (trace-only).  NAMED COST: a genuine single-band L1/L2 slip on an L5-chain reference arc loses its narrowing because its only silent pair is the untested one (all-band = the pre-card response).  SPEC_event_harness s22.
- **evidence:** FP-1 (SPEC_event_harness s20.2, 2026-09-13; the s20 matrix re-run on the warm-resuming field seed evseed_field6h_v5 ep0604, legs runs/evleg_fp1_*): G-A reproduces every pre-registered row (E1 {L5X}; E2 all = the clause's cost; E3/G24 all via ref_arc = the trap closed; E3/G25 all via the (1,5) veto) and the G25 control is clean, BUT if_phase_td_pairs=all fans a slip ON a chain's reference satellite out to every other satellite of that chain (G24 = both L5 chains' reference at GOPE: E1 six false refloats on G11/G25/G32 at 253-1580x the threshold, an innocent satellite's phase bias re-realized network-wide by 13.7 m, consumer mean |d3D| 4.71 vs 0.98 m; no backstop - card R tests the first chain only, T4 = 0, no scope row to narrow) => G-B/G-C/G-D FAIL on every G24 leg; NOT pinned.  Counter-weight: at +39 a real 46-sigma (1,5) slip at JPLM/G10 is caught by all and missed by first (pair-rotation pool restart).  The fix is REF-FAN (s22.6, network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_arm_all); the pin is re-run once it passes F1-F4.  FIELD IDENTITY (SPEC_event_harness s22.4, 2026-09-12, runs/evleg_cbref_*, seed runs/evseed_field6h ep0604 on the s21.5 interim posture state_snapshot_carry:false, exe 5BD7D2DC): B1 OFF byte-identical (null on/off products 1FF0BA20; 12 of 1992 event rows differ, 12/12 carry ref_arc); B2 the equal-count L1C+29 & L5X+29 slip on GOPE00CZE/G24 (the reference of both L5 chains) goes from keep implicated=L2W (the s20.1 trap: the innocent band freed, 29 cycles left on both guilty bands) to all-band with iftd(L1C+L5X)=ref_arc;iftd(L2W+L5X)=ref_arc named on the row and the IF-TD refloat no longer holding L1C back (cb_kept gone); E1 L5X -99 on G24 {L5X} on both arms, products DEF230D7 identical; E2 L1C +37 on G24 all-band (the named cost; OFF keep L1C); E3 on G25 (not a reference) byte-identical to OFF (44D461B0).  Run-end ref-arc refusals 6 on the null, 8 on E3/E2 at G24, attributing exactly by pair.  B3 4 forfeited of 106 narrowings across the leg pairs (3.8 %), all four injected, 0 natural; the clause also resolves 6 inconsistent fallbacks per leg into narrowings with products unchanged.  Reference exposure under pairs=all: 18.3 % of IF-TD samples, 107/507 arcs.  B4 SIM STORM ARM (runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_refarc = the increment-1 sts arm + the knob, exe cbref, 720/720, 1507 s) vs runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_sts: products byte-identical for the first 283 epochs (first actuation at tow 310890), then within noise on every graded channel - ZWD settled 0.0059 / 0.0059 m, consumer settled p50 0.0807 / 0.0807 (rms 0.09006 -> 0.09004), T4 attribution 11 / 11, split-referenced 0.0 / 0.0 per epoch, WL 99.2, EWL 100.0, L1C ref-free 90.4 / 90.4, IF-TD vetoes 116 / 116, handovers 462 (362 continuous / 100 cold) both; ref-arc refusals 29 [L1C_L5Q 12, L1C_L7Q 5, L2W_L5Q 12], inconsistent 202 -> 186 (13 -> no-exoneration, 3 -> narrowed).  Every s22.3 B4 bar passes; the sim bed never carries the trap, so the clause is neutral there and fixing on the field.
- **interlock:** REQUIRES network.cycle_slip.common_band_scope (loader warns + disarms otherwise).  Arm WITH if_phase_td_pairs = all (the veto's coverage) exactly as the scope's own interlock says.  Not fingerprinted: it changes which silences may exonerate, never a carried value's meaning.
- **armed when:** `on("network.cycle_slip.common_band_ref_arc")`  with network.cycle_slip.common_band_scope wherever it is armed (the sim golden).  On the field golden the scope itself carries a documented exception (s20.1: the equal-count trap this clause closes, and no warm-resuming seed to price the wrong narrowing), so the clause is out of regime there; the field pin of scope + pairs + clause rides on the RS-2 seed (s21.5) once the s20 wire/consumer bars can be read.

### `network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_arm_all`
- **default:** false at src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp ~4917 (jval "network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_arm_all"; warn + disarm without pairs = all or without if_phase_td_ref_gate ~5108); detector src/core/slip/IfPhaseSlipDetector.cpp (the chain loop); run-end line in NetworkTeardown.cpp; merged main e6b04a4 (worker 9bd2d51).  — **regime:** `on("network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_pairs") and on("network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_gate")`.  **RECOMMEND_SCOPED.**  Lineages running if_phase_td_pairs = all with the empirical reference gate.  On an additional IF-TD chain: an OWN-elected reference is tested by the reference arm through the empirical gate with that chain's own dispersion state (never the legacy fixed floor - the s14.10.3 flood); an ADOPTED reference inherits the first chain's verdict (this pair's reference arc reset, the pooled fan-out suppressed).  Per-chain counters + run-end line.  Inert on pairs = first.  Not fingerprinted (a detector decision).
- **evidence:** REF-FAN (SPEC_event_harness s22.6/s22.7, 2026-09-13; legs reffan_{null,e1,e5,e3g24,e3g25}_{off,on} from evseed_field6h_v5 ep0604 on the PIN posture scope + pairs all + the clause, one harness, exe 4C40B93A): F1 the flood is held by card R's empirical gate - additional-chain reference-arm fires 1 (a natural G27/SCTB00ATA reference slip) vs the first chain's 1 over 60 epochs, 93 of 94 legacy candidates on the wide (L2W,L5Q)/(L2W,L5X) chains suppressed; F2 the fan-out is gone - collateral IF-TD refloats at GOPE/G24 6 -> 0 on E1, E5 and E3/G24 (the reference's own arcs reset instead), the innocent 13.3 m G32/L1C phase-bias re-realization removed on all three, consumer mean |d3D| over +30..+50 1.94 -> 0.72 / 2.20 -> 1.01 / 1.34 -> 0.99, blast radius 7.5x smaller; the G24 band-scope verdicts unchanged; F3 the G25 control's detections bit-identical; F4 smoke 87a33bf0 both arms on the field golden (pairs first: inert), fix-off legs byte-identical to FP-1's PIN legs, suite 2036 / 277 / 0.  Misses to re-read on a second realization: consumer MAX |d3D| E5 2.93 -> 4.47 (+53 %) and E1 +3 %; the E3/G24 station clock at +45 -1.19 -> +1.50 m.  The ADOPTED-reference (inherited) path never fired on this lineage (the L5 chains own-elect) - unvalidated on the field.  Pinned nowhere yet: the field golden runs pairs first; the sim golden's storm arm is owed (exception).
- **interlock:** Disarmed (warned) unless if_phase_td_pairs = all AND if_phase_td_ref_gate; the detector refuses the legacy candidate condition on an additional chain.
- **armed when:** `on("network.cycle_slip.if_phase_td_ref_arm_all")`  every pairs = all lineage once its bed is validated: the field pin rides FP-2 (the s20 matrix + twin on the fixed posture, from the v8 seed); the sim golden (pairs all pinned) carries an exception until its storm arm (s22.5's B4 protocol) is run.

### `network.cycle_slip.refloat_band_scope`  (all | implicated)
- **default:** "all" = byte-identical shipped behaviour - MAIN 5fee8cb (merged 2026-09-10 18:56; ledger rows 2026-09-10 19:05) (branch worktree-evharness 46284b0 + 798a2e6, 2026-09-10).  Loader src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (jval network.cycle_slip.refloat_band_scope, hard error on any other string); enum NetworkRefloatBandScope + field cycle_slip_refloat_band_scope in include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp; decision in src/core/slip/NetworkSlipQc.cpp on the MW/GF per-band decomposition of RawSlipObs / RawSlipVerdict (dN1 = (dGF - lambda2*dMW)/(lambda1 - lambda2), lambda2 recovered from the pair's own lambda_WL), evaluated PER EPOCH against this drain's bands plus what earlier drains already latched (relevel->free_ambs, carry_slipped_ambs); counters incl. bscope_too_late and the all-band FALLBACK; same run-end clause and test file as raim_erase_band_scope.  — **regime:** `on("network.phase_leveling") and not on("network.carry_invariant")`.  **KEEP_OFF — falsified on the harness and the storm bed.**  When a slip detector refloats a whole satellite, free only the bands the raw MW/GF evidence implicates and keep one band's carried level pinned.  FALSIFIED: a detector refloat fires on POSITIVE raw evidence, so keeping a band is a bet against the detector, and on a whole-satellite loss of lock (LLI on every band - the common field case) the kept band moved too.  ⚠ Build lesson the harness caught: scoping each drain independently was BYTE-IDENTICAL to control (the LLI drain kept L1C, then the IF-TD driver freed it) - the invariant is per epoch, not per drain.
- **evidence:** HARNESS (Study 3, own null): single-band slip control identical to three decimals on every column (the knob does not perturb an already correctly scoped detector response); genuine ALL-BAND slip x3 cycles on S21000SIM/G10: level floor 0.498 -> 7.658 cyc (15x WORSE; L2W 0.665 -> 9.763, L5Q 0.700 -> 10.197), slant iono -0.089 -> -1.302 m, ZWD +4.0 -> +25.8 mm - the kept L1C really moved 10 cycles and the other two bands re-tapped onto it.  STORM BED (combined arm runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_bscope, 6 h): detector 1,091 arcs -> 1,091 freed / 1,091 kept with 1,091 ALL-BAND FALLBACKS and 0 decisions on raw evidence (on this bed a whole-arc implication is LLI on every band, whose primary-pair step is unusable, so 'implicated' ran as 'keep L1C whatever happened to it'); ref-free WL 86.2 -> 56.9 %, consumer p50 1.08 -> 2.89 m, ambient 1.74 -> 2.97 cyc - the integer collapse is the size of the fallback count.  The honest handling of a whole-satellite loss of lock is the LANE-PRESERVING refloat (SPEC_lane_slip_response §7: re-level the kept band from the EWL-MW snap, sigma ~0.05 cyc), which does not exist yet.
- **interlock:** Requires phase_leveling; inert under carry_invariant.  Not fingerprinted.  Byte-identical at 'all'.  Do not arm with raim_erase_band_scope in an A/B - the combined storm arm is confounded by this knob's fallbacks (§Z.6); grade each against its own ON null on the harness.
### `network.cycle_slip.oracle_true_slip`  (`repair` | `free`)
- **default:** `"repair"` — `src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp` (jval beside `network.cycle_slip.oracle_false_alarm`, hard error on any other value); struct `GnssConfigTypes.hpp` `SlipQcConfig` `oracle_*`; mechanism `NetworkSlipOracle.{hpp,cpp}`; per-tier false-alarm counters `NetworkTeardown.cpp`.  **MAIN, commit `3dd1284`** (2026-09-10).  — **regime:** `on("network.cycle_slip.oracle_relevel")`.  **SIM-ONLY DIAGNOSTIC — it reads the fixture's `truth_slips.csv`**; never a posture, never a golden, never a field lineage.  The second axis of the ORACLE 2×2: `repair` is §13.5's ceiling (shift the carried level by the true Σδ·λ), `free` DECLINES the oracle for true slips so the caller runs today's ordinary free/re-level.  With `false_alarm: keep` it isolates exactly one population — actuations with NO truth slip keep their level, every real slip pays the shipped response.  The fourth corner (both `free`) is a pure no-op and the loader WARNs rather than refuses, because that corner is the hook's byte-identity control.
- **evidence (ONE ARM, decisive in the direction OPPOSITE to the one it was built to test — `docs/SPEC_slip_qc_hardening.md` §13.7, SPEC_amb_iono §6.19b, §7 item 41):** context first (`scripts/simnet_slip_census.py`, in-block arm, post-Card-R goldens): of the slipped bed's 2 486 truth slips **2 337 (94.0 %) are DETECTED** — MW 2 006 / IF-TD 331 / T4 59 / raim 16 — and 74 (3.0 %) are SILENT (mostly ≥ 90-cycle jumps at el ≈ 10°), so detection is not the problem; but of **4 708 slip-tier events, 2 371 (50 %) have NO truth slip in the window — MW 1 132, IF-TD 1 239** ≈ **6.4 false re-levels per station-hour against 6.8 true slips**.  THE ARM `runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_orafa` (`true_slip: free` + `false_alarm: keep`) vs `runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk`: **6 804 false alarms KEPT** (MW 4 156 / IF-TD 2 131 / LLI 517), 3 409 true slips freed as today.  ZWD truth rms 1.188 → 1.066 m (−10 %) but p50 0.048 → **0.104**; consumer settled p50 1.085 → **2.92 m**, rms 2.389 → **4.46 m (2.7× WORSE)**; T4 arc erases 29 613 → **48 078 (+62 %)**.  **FAIL.**  ⭐ **The finding: the false-alarm re-levels are a RESYNC mechanism** — on this bed the detectors' 50 % false-alarm rate is doing useful work by accident, dragging drifting arcs back into their station's current (walking) frame.  ⇒ **"tighten the detector thresholds on this bed" is dead as a card**, and with §6.17b (T4 mostly re-erases the damage its own previous re-level created) BOTH accuracy-of-response levers are measured: the response's ACCURACY is not what the consumer buys from it.  ⚠ **Counting basis, never to be mixed in a rate:** the census counts EVENTS per (station, satellite) (~2 300 false per 6 h); the oracle counters count per-signal ACTUATIONS (6 804); the retired "13 485 actuations" figure was the per-signal OFFER count.  ⚠ This false-alarm rate is a property of THIS BED (fixture phase noise ~5× the field's, MW thresholds field-empirical — §6.14.3); the same probe on the field is unowned.
- **interlock:** Inherits every `oracle_relevel` interlock — warn-and-DISARM under `carry_invariant` and without `phase_leveling`; HARD ERROR on a missing/unreadable `oracle_truth_root`, an unknown policy string, a negative `oracle_noise_cyc`, or any roster station lacking `truth_slips.csv`.  Independent of `oracle_false_alarm`; the `free`+`free` corner is a deliberate no-op (warn, not refuse).  ⚠ §13.5's "26 % detected but KEPT" class must be re-read in the light of this knob — part of that class is this policy.
### `network.raim_k_sigma`  (value knob, code default 6.0)
- **default:** 6.0 — `src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:4428-4429` (`jval network.raim_k_sigma` / `network_raim_k_sigma`); echoed in the RAIM posture summary at `:9121` beside `network.raim_min_stations`; the per-tier run-end flag-rate table prints it as `k=6.0 => implied Gaussian a 1.973e-09`.  — **regime:** `on("network.raim")`.  **KEEP the shipped 6.0.**  This row exists because the value was probed as the *more-erasing* end of the erase-rate family (`network.raim_arc_persist_epochs` is the *less-erasing* end) and the BRACKET is the durable result.
- **evidence (ONE ARM, 2026-09-10 — SPEC_amb_iono §6.19a, §7 item 40):** `runs/golden_sim_campaign_zwdblk_k4` = the in-block control with `raim_k_sigma` 6 → 4 and nothing else (slipped bed, 6 h, whole-run `simnet_grade`).  T4 arc erases 29 613 → **46 575 (+57 %)** on an identical 645 406-opportunity denominator — emphatically not inert.  PRODUCER improves: ZWD truth rms 1.188 → **0.972 m (−18 %)**, L1C settled ref-free med|e| 1.77 → 1.67 cyc.  CONSUMER pays: settled p50 1.085 → **1.317 m (+21 %)**, rms 2.389 → **3.26 m (+36 %)**; ZWD p50 0.048 → 0.055; sat-clock contrast 11.79 → 14.4 m.  **FAIL** against its pre-registration (consumer p50 ≤ 0.8 PASS / ≥ 1.0 FAIL).  ⭐ **The bracket:** read against the `raim_arc_persist_epochs` K ladder on the SAME posture (K = 10 / 40 / 1e6 → consumer settled p50 2.296 / 2.729 / 2.320 m, i.e. ×2.1–×2.5 worse with FEWER erases), k = 4 is the more-erasing end and it is ×1.2 worse — **the consumer optimum of the erase-rate family is the shipped corner, in BOTH directions**, so no value of either knob tunes out of the station-frame walk.  Carry-forward: do not re-run this ladder on a storm bed — it is a single-event question for the §7 item 43 harness.
- **interlock:** Shared by every post-fit tier, so a change moves T3, T3c and T4 together and no A/B off it isolates one tier.  It does NOT touch the PREFIT census (`network.trace_prefit_census` carries its own k) nor the within-epoch tier's own gate.  ⚠ **Erase counts are only comparable across arms at an identical opportunity denominator** — quote "N flag of M opp" from the run-end flag-rate table, not the count alone (the §6.16.7d rate-vs-count trap).
### `network.ambiguity_resolution.trace`  (the Phase-2 producer-AR diagnostic's per-DD trace)
- **default:** false — `src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp` (`jval network.ambiguity_resolution.trace`, requires `.enable`, loader warns otherwise); stream ownership + `kProducerArHeader` in `NetworkRunTraces.{hpp,cpp}` on the `dd_frac_trace` pattern; fix → basis mapping `netar::mapFixToBasis`; run-end clause `Network (producer AR, Phase 2 log-only): …` in `NetworkTeardown.cpp`; provenance keys `ambiguity_resolution.{enable,trace,ils_max_dim}` join the manifest only when armed.  **MAIN, commit `dd6c81c`** (2026-09-10).  — **regime:** `False` in the ledger (**deliberately NOT a posture row**; its real predicate `on("network.ambiguity_resolution.enable")` is kept in `regime_prose`).  A POSTURE line for it would nag every FIELD and production run to arm a diagnostic that costs 54 % of the epoch at 60 stations and writes rows nobody without truth can grade — the same shape as `network.product_flush_per_epoch`'s vacuous row.  **RECOMMEND_SCOPED as the Phase-2 INSTRUMENT on a sim truth bed** — SCOPED, not ON: it is only meaningful where `.enable` is armed AND truth exists to join it to, and `.enable`'s search costs 54 % of the epoch at 60 stations.  Never arm the pair on a field or production lineage.  TRACE-CLASS and READ-ONLY: Phase 2 does not re-pin and does not re-solve, so the emitted product is byte-identical to the unarmed run.  It writes `logs/network_producer_ar.csv` — **one row per DD of the WHOLE basis, not just the searched subset**, because the fixability CEILING is a statement about the population the `ils_max_dim` cap chose *from*.
- **evidence (FIRST GRADED ARM, 2026-09-10 — SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount §6.19e, §7 item 44):** `runs/golden_sim_netar` (`config/test_suite/_golden_sim_netar.json` = the sim golden VERBATIM + the AR block + run paths; quiet 60-sta truth bed `runs/simnet60_quiet_wu93_24h`, 24 h, 2 879 epochs, exit 0).  **The instrument works:** 852 260 rows, **100 % joinable to truth**, run-end clause present and honest (`2869 epochs searched, 298 DD p50 / 200 searched p50, fixed epochs 2238 (78.0%), DD fixed total 29828, ratio p50 12.60, p_succ p50 1.0000, med_DD_sigma p50 0.3170 cyc; carry-blind; trace on`).  ⭐ **What it measured is the row's real content: NO-GO FOR PHASE 3.**  Settled h12–24 vs the arm's own pre-registration — fixed epochs **86.8 % PASS**; P1 rows **PASS**; searched-subset |e| p50 **0.198 cyc FAIL** (gate ≤ 0.10); CORRECT-fix rate among kept **85.52 %** (13 328 / 15 585) **FAIL** (gate ≥ 99 %) = a **14.48 % wrong-fix rate, 14× the 1 % threshold the pre-registration named as the carry-aware trigger**.  Every one of the 200 largest-|e| wrong fixes is off by **exactly ±1 cycle** (|e| 0.90–1.10 cyc) at formal σ median **0.091 cyc** — median **|e|/σ = 10.4** — accepted at ratio 14.0 and `p_succ` **1.0000**.  **The diagnostic tell: correctness gets WORSE as the run settles** (93.28 % h0–12 → 85.52 %) while the gate's ratio p50 RISES (11.31 → 14.01) — a covariance shrinking faster than the error it describes, which is what a carry-blind conditional block does on the phase-leveling vehicle.  Fixability ceiling 72.4 % settled (plain rounding wrong on 27.6 % of the tightest DDs the cap chose); Galileo worse than GPS on both (79.6 / 81.7 % vs 90.4 / 90.3 %).  Cost **2 152 ms of a 3 984 ms epoch (54 %)** at 60 sta, ~1.7 h extra wall on a 24 h arm.  **NEXT, a prerequisite and not an option: `network.carry_aware_covariance` applied to the Phase-2 DD block, re-graded with this same instrument, BEFORE any Phase-3 build.**
- **interlock:** Requires `network.ambiguity_resolution.enable`.  ⚠ **The trace is only interpretable against TRUTH** (`scripts/simnet_producer_ar_grade.py`); on a field lineage it writes rows nobody can grade.  ⚠ **Two indexing traps the implementation handles and any reader must respect:** `IlsResult::kept` indexes the CAPPED, σ-PERMUTED search vector while `afix` is in *kept* order — `netar::mapFixToBasis` does the double indirection and is unit-tested (`tests/test_producer_ar_trace.cpp`) with the naive mapping as an explicit negative control, because getting it wrong yields a well-formed CSV with the integers on the wrong satellites and nothing downstream can tell.  ⚠ **The file's DDs share ONE reference pair per (constellation, signal), and it rotates** — 213 / 261 reference-satellite and 242 / 313 reference-station switches over 2 869 epochs on the first arm — so any per-band or per-station split off this trace compares rotating populations (§6.12).  The run-end clause carries invariant-#4 branches for "armed but the ILS ran on zero epochs" and "trace armed but zero rows", plus a WARN whenever a fix is accepted on a carry-BLIND covariance — the warning that fired throughout the first arm.

Against the white population CRLB F3 sits at 1.44× (hv 1.24×); rows with all legs born mid-run sit AT the bound — the excess is ignition-born legs (1.25-1.34×, no logged event) and legs hit by a cold PB-gauge handover (~1.4×).  Run-end counter (invariant #4, `main_log.log:803`): `21180 block(s) whitened, 950551 row(s) emitted, 8534 column(s) marginalized at inject, 0 pair(s) zeroed, 0 column(s) refused, 0 factorization fallback(s); block dim histogram <10:0 10-24:221 25-49:15834 50-74:5125 75+:0 (max 66); within-epoch refloat re-whiten 0 ok / 0 failed`.  **Byte identity (invariant #6) MEASURED:** sha256 of `products.txt` identical across `nonint_base3h` (2026-09-03 exe), `nonint_base3h_f1exe` and `nonint_base3h_f3exe` (F3 exe, both knobs OFF) — `1c1b60c7…29229d`, 8,601,257 bytes.  **Wire grade (§6.8.4): IMPROVES** — settled ZWD rms 0.416 → **0.0088 m** (p50 5.5 mm, |err|/σ 0.94, every station < 2.1 cm; 47× base, 22× F1), sat code bias 0.814 → 0.507 m and rx-DCB 1.587 → 1.226 m (= F1), rx clock contrast 1.94 → 1.68 m, post-fit code σ̂0 1.70 → 1.10 with 0 |s| > 6, T3/T4 false alarms 5188 → 1, the DD-fractional trace leaves uniform for the first time (circR 0.016 → 0.15 / 0.21); **the one channel WORSE than F1 is the satellite clock** (rms 1.583 vs 1.437 m, p50 1.011 vs 0.832; G16 / G09 / G18 tails at base level) with a base-like over-confident URA (0.005 m, vf 1.1e5) — better than base, a regression on F1 (SPEC §7 item 18).  Consumer (SZZ000SIM, float, AR 0/360): settled 3D rms 0.414 → 0.295 m at 1e-4 (= F1), **0.245 m at 1e-2 — the best arm**, up bias +0.19 → +0.04.  +7.4 % ms/epoch (uncontrolled load).  Posture: **the arm to carry forward is F3 + `phase_datum_handover_max_var_m2` 1e-2** (`config/test_suite/_nonint_losblock3h_hv.json`); sim-only — the 24 h arm (pre-registered §6.8.6: 0.14-0.15 cyc / 84-86 %) and the field arm are owed.

Per-LOS: the elevation-shaped station term in the frame-removed iono error is 60 % of the variance under F1 (its slope tracking the station's ZWD error at corr −0.83) and 10 % under F3 (corr +0.14).  The population-CRLB instrument (`r4resid/crlb_pop.py`: white, ρ′ known, I free per epoch, each leg's information over its present epochs) is the standing grade: med |e|/σ_crlb 12.2 → 5.58 → **0.970** → **0.839** against the bound 0.674; the CRLB mixture does NOT fall with run length (24 h bed h2-3 0.145 / 86.1 % vs h12-24 0.137 / 86.4 %, min-leg age p50 117 vs 119), so "the gap is age" is FALSIFIED — the 3 h settled quarter is already the steady-state age mix.  ZWD (`r4resid/a4`): formal 0.0057 m settled, error rms 0.0089 / med 0.0050 m at k = 359, D(1/10/30/100) 0.0051 / 0.0064 / 0.0075 / 0.0089 m, |err|/σ 1.0 from k = 240.  Handover ceiling (§6.8.3): at 1e-4 m² F3 is refused exactly like F1 (0/93 continuous, 7 settled 4-15 m PB steps 7/7 at cold-handover tows, 1653 carries erased); at 1e-2 m² 70/93 continuous — every refusal a successor ≤ 52 min old in h0/h1 (an AGE gate under honest σ), settled window 21/21 continuous, 0 settled PB steps > 1 m, settled phase-side steps > 0.05 m 78 (base 47, F3@1e-4 200), step rms 0.0111 m (base 0.0104), carries erased 408 (base 57) — PASS on every settled-window continuity criterion except the carry count; wire accuracy neutral to the ceiling (clock / CB / rx-DCB identical to 3 digits).  The final-40-min consumer walk (east −0.15 → −0.32 m from the G12 re-emission epoch ~267930, wire flat) is present on every F arm INCLUDING hv with zero settled cold handovers — not the handovers (SPEC §7 item 19).

Supersedes `phase_leveling_los_scale` on every whitened block (the F1 scale applies only on a counted factorization fallback — 0 in 21,180 blocks measured).  Invariants #1/#7: a band-scoped free-epoch latch / pending reseed / T4 erase / gap prune / pin MARGINALIZES the column (8,534 at inject over 3 h, lumped — the per-reason breakdown is owed, SPEC §7 item 16); a column's RETURN is a re-coupling edge at its harvested variance; the within-epoch RAIM refloat re-whitens survivors but was never exercised on data (T2 354 opportunities / 0 flags — uncertified).  Harvest re-stashes the full block every epoch (0 harvest-stamp mismatches).  Fingerprinted (`pl:losblock`): fresh convergence only.  Known residues it does NOT fix: the rx-CB direction's 2× over-count (block over ambiguity columns only + a separate diagonal rx-CB carry — DD-invisible above the bound at 3 h, rx-DCB formal 0.067 = base's 0.066; SPEC §7 item 17), the ignition-born-leg excess 1.25-1.34× (§7 item 8), the satellite-clock lag behind F1 (§7 item 18).  The T3/T4 pool sees its dense L⁻¹ rows: post-fit pool census unchanged, RAIM-attrib pool σ̂0 0.23 → 0.99 with 0 acted — admission is inferred, not read (§7 item 4).  Sim-validated only.
- **24 h sim verdict (2026-09-05, SPEC §6.9):** full 24 h L1C DD med|e| 0.283 cyc / 64 % at the true integer (base 2.87 / 10 %), quiet hours 0.18–0.23 / 74–83 %, mature chords 0.16 / 89 % (= the pre-registered reading), lanes WL 98 % / EWL 100 % all day — but the settled quarter reads 0.425 / 53 % because of two ~4 h NETWORK-WIDE degradation episodes trailing the eclipse re-admission clusters (clean chords 0.19 → 0.58–0.68 cyc), ~2× the same edge under F1; the handover ceiling is not the lever for them (arm at 1e-4 reproduces them; it IS the lever for the consumer: h5–h7 1.9–2.6 m vs 1.1–1.4 at 1e-2 vs base 0.12–0.48).  **Stays default-OFF for settled work until the block's re-coupling edge at satellite re-admission is built (invariant #7).**
- **FIELD arm (2026-09-09, SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount §6.15, `runs/fldf3_{ci,pl,on}`, FLD63/EXT63 chain, 61 GPS-dual stations + SH iono, 12 h fresh):** F3 fires and stays conditioned on real data (86,344 blocks, 0 fallbacks, 0 refused) and MITIGATES its vehicle's divergence on every scale instrument (ZWD sink 120 → 55 m per-station span, DD-iono wander 3.7 → 0.77 m, DD-ambiguity wander 24 → 7 cyc, T4a 10.6k → 3.6k, float cold consumer 4.4 → 2.9 m rms), but the phase-leveling VEHICLE does not converge on this chain (S-basis null-space gate withholds from epoch 101 on both leveling arms, ZWD sink 10² m) and the pre-registered headline is FALSIFIED: the field DD fractions stay at the uniform null on L1C, L2W and WL12 (residual ≳ 3 L1 cyc), read on a non-converged arm.  **Field verdict: KEEP OFF on field lineages until a leveling posture CONVERGES there** (⚠ decided on producer-internal convergence — 223 withheld epochs, a 55 m ZWD sink, prires |t| 1.5e8 — even though the F3 arm gave the BEST float-consumer wire of the three, 2.9 m vs the shipped control's 6.6 m; a producer that withholds a sixth of its epochs is not shippable, and every field wire here is metre-class anyway, so the DD fractions at the null are what a ~35-cycle wire must produce, not an independent finding) (eq34 chain datum + SBC + SH-pinned iono + leveling was never validated; the sim golden's vehicle runs the prioritized pivot phase datum); probes `_fldf3_pl_{pdp3h,nosbc3h}` queued.  Independent field finding from the same ladder: the shipped carry_invariant posture's per-epoch ZWD is a metre-class sink and (I, N₁, N₂, ZWD) co-move along a weakly-determined direction (§6.15.3a item 5), so the field control's DD "ambiguities" are not estimable-invariant in practice.  Sim regime and RECOMMEND_SCOPED status unchanged.
- **FIELD arm, SECOND ROUND (2026-09-09 19:20, SPEC_amb_iono §6.15.4, `runs/fldf3_{pl2,on2}`): on the CONVERGING leveling posture (found by the probe ladder: `carry_coupling_gauge_project: true` + the sim golden's phase-datum family — `phase_datum_eq34: false`, `phase_datum_prioritized`, `phase_datum_handover_continuous`, `phase_anchor_carry_reref`, `phase_datum_min_stations: 2` — 1,440/1,440 solved, 0 withholds) F3 is SOUND (87,140 blocks, 0 fallbacks, +1.4 % D0) and gives the first non-uniform DD fractions the field has produced — WL12 R 0.562 vs null 0.450, near-integer 24.3 %, ref-free gap −0.028 — while L1C/L2W stay at the null (R − null +0.019 / +0.011, below the pre-registered +0.05) and the float cold consumer is flat (3.99 vs 3.77 m): the pre-registered PARTIAL branch.  Residual per-band wander under F3 0.71 cyc, iono-shaped (cross-band 1.17, the sim F3 arm's family), vs 0.12 on the posture without F3.  **Field status: KEEP OFF as a promotion decision (no consumer win; prires |t| ~1e8 on both arms), field REGIME now defined (the converging leveling posture above).**  The field lever the ladder actually found is that posture, not F3: it retires the shipped posture's (I, N, ZWD) trade (DD-tropo wander 1.22 → 0.07 m, ambiguity↔iono co-motion slope 1.72 → 0.03, DD wander 10 → 0.12 cyc) and halves the float-consumer error (6.6 → 3.8 m) — a VEHICLE question for the field goldens, owed its own card.

### `network.phase_datum_handover_max_var_m2`  (the PB-gauge continuous-handover ledger ceiling — NOTE, not a promotion row)
- **note (2026-09-04, round 4):** loader default 1.0 m² (GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:6454-6456; banner "keep this TIGHT" :7683-7688); the FT / camp60wu sticky lineages set **1e-4 m²** (σ = 1 cm), a value sized against the OVER-COUNTED leveling σ (SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount §6.8.3; gate NetworkPhaseDatum.cpp:376-403, var = the successor's border-exact conditional).  Under any honest-σ carry (F1, F3, var floor) it refuses every successor: 0/93 continuous, 1653 leveling carries erased (base 57), 4-15 m PB frame steps on the settled wire matched 7/7 to the cold-handover tows.  **1e-2 m² measured:** F3 70/93 (refusals = arcs ≤ 52 min old, settled window fully continuous, wire accuracy neutral, consumer 0.295 → 0.245 m) / F1 67/93 (per-BAND refusals on arcs up to 3 h old, 4 settled > 1 m steps, consumer +0.43 m up bias — INVALID pairing); neither ≥ 90/93.  Re-express relative to the successor's own conditional σ (or age-aware), with the 378-σ unvalidated-target guard (SPEC_pivot_visibility_gauge_defect §5.0) re-demonstrated, before F1 / F3 are armed on any lineage; until then 1e-2 is the F3 working value only.

## Tier 4 — KEEP_OFF  (diagnostic, falsified, or superseded)
### `estimation.adaptive_iono_process_noise.enable`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoad.cpp:1250-1251 (default false; struct AdaptiveIonoQConfig::enabled=false at include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp:1738)  — **regime:** STRUCTURALLY INERT on the network producer and in real-time. Wired only on the user-side post-processed PPP/PPP-RTK path (Application::run), where it is untested on field data.
- **evidence:** Grep of every call site: IonoVariabilityMonitor is constructed at src/core/app/Application.cpp:1524-1526 and nowhere else; zero references in ApplicationNetwork*/Network* seams. RealTimeEngine.cpp:284-290 explicitly warns "adaptive_iono_process_noise is enabled but not wired into the streaming pipeline — per-sat iono process noise will stay static" (corroborated by project_realtime_model_degradation). For the user-side path the only endorsement on file is project_config_toggle_gotchas' "Wired wins (safe to enable)" list — a 2026-06-27 code-tracing audit, NOT a measurement — so on that path it is UNVALIDATED, not validated-good. Note project_cv_slant_iono records it was repointed to inflate iono_rate_psd as a RATIO when enable_iono_rate is on, so its meaning depends on another default-OFF knob.
- **interlock:** Sub-knobs recent_window (:1252-1253, 20), baseline_window (:1254-1255, 240), max_multiplier (:1256-1257, 100.0), min_epochs (:1259-1260, 30), trace (:1261-1262, false) are all inert while this is false. Meaning of the multiplier changes with estimation.enable_iono_rate.

- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoad.cpp:1261-1262 (default false)  — **regime:** Diagnostic CSV writer (per-satellite GF/TD2/variability), user-side only.

### `estimation.estimate_ifb (standalone, network mode)`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoad.cpp:787 (false)
- **evidence:** Arming it standalone in network mode is actively destructive; it is only correct as a forced companion of W6 or GLONASS. project_glonass_ifb_network.md: 'GPS/Galileo non-reference-band IFB (the surprise). Forcing estimate_ifb=true to get GLONASS IFB ALSO created GPS/Gal per-band IFB/DCB states. A non-reference-band IFB has no IFB-free reference code to anchor slant iono, so it confounds iono<->IFB<->ambiguity -> a ~40-dim diffuse null ... FIX = in network mode allocate ONLY the GLONASS per-slot IFB.' And the failure is whole-station: 'a singular station is DROPPED ENTIRELY — its good GPS/Gal rows are lost too'. project_network_code_bias_scope.md trap 1 gives the W6 version: 'Non-primary rx IFB columns must be DROPPED in splitStation ... the L5Q PHASE-bias common mode collapsed 1e-2 -> 5e-6 and withheld EVERY epoch.'
- **interlock:** FORCED ON by network.estimate_sat_code_bias (:820) and by network.estimate_glonass (:1027). Only 16 of 350 configs set it true, and those are the W6/GLONASS arms.
### `estimation.global_vtec_sh.trace`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoad.cpp:1320-1321 (default false; struct GnssConfigTypes.hpp:1713)  — **regime:** Diagnostic-only (writes network_vtec_sh.csv + network_vtec_sh_health.csv); never touches the solve. Keep OFF for production emission runs, arm it for any run you intend to GRADE — every current field config sets it true.

Not a solve knob, so there is nothing to promote; the only cost is I/O. Flagged here because it is the sole path to the per-degree Kaula-ratio / a00 readout the SH gradings depend on.
### `network.apriori_clock_anchor_sigma_m`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:935 (0.0 = auto, mode-resolved)
- **evidence:** 0 (auto) is the validated value; an explicit override is the documented way to break the broadcast-anchored rank gate. Loader: 'The brdc_rts auto default is 3.0 m (1/sigma^2 ~ 0.11, safe at any realistic scale); only an explicit override can be this weak' and 'a ~305-station network rejects sigma >~ 60 m as under-specified and withholds every epoch'. Structural caveat still open, project_network_ssystem_dia_assessment.md #6: 'A-priori clock anchor is a permanent every-epoch Tikhonov blend toward CODE, with no decay. Either declare it as a product convention ... or decay it.' No measurement supports any non-auto value.

### `network.carry_vce_apply`
- **default:** include/config/GnssConfig.hpp (network_carry_vce_apply_{false}); parsed GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp next to carry_vce
- **verdict:** UNVALIDATED
- **regime:** BUILT 2026-08-04 (docs/SPEC_carry_vce_apply.md) — no longer a load error. LASER 5-95 variance-FACTOR iteration: per (carry family x constellation) the carry fold variance is multiplied by an alpha2 that tracks the M2 monitor's robust_floor / hand_fold_var, INFLATE-ONLY (alpha2 >= 1, so the invariant-#1 lock-in direction is structurally unreachable), slew-limited (1.05/epoch), ceilinged (1000), and frozen on contaminated or under-populated epochs. SOLVE-AFFECTING and FINGERPRINTED; a warm resume with it armed is a HARD RUN ERROR (alpha2 is cross-epoch and deliberately unsnapshotted, so v1 is fresh-convergence-only). Companion knobs carry_vce_apply_{families,slew,max,min_rows}.
- **evidence:** UNIT: tests/test_carry_vce_apply.cpp, 10 gates (convergence to a known 25x ratio with sigma_hat_post -> 1; inflate-only never below 1 at any epoch; slew/ceiling/freeze engage AND release with edges; no cross-family or cross-constellation leakage; anti-ratchet round trip against the window's closed form; unarmed instances exactly 1.0). FIELD: none — the section 7 acceptance (calibration ratio on 3 h/305 + a 12 h window) is untouched. WIRING SMOKE ONLY (63 sta, 1 h, DOY-129, same-build OFF/ON, configs config/test_suite/_vceapply_smoke_{off,on}.json): alpha2 climbed 1 -> 3.06 (peak 3.97) and then TRACKED, sigma_hat_post pinned at 1.000000 for ~80 epochs; products differ, so the factor demonstrably reaches the fold sites. TWO READING TRAPS recorded there: (a) the honest factor was 3.1x, not the 479x hand-FLOOR ratio the monitor prints — most rows fold at var_prev + q*dt, far above the floor, and M2's '30-120x' numbers are floor ratios; (b) the ON arm removed all 44 A4 sigma0 withholds the OFF control took, which section 7.2 explicitly says to treat as a RED FLAG (a factor that ate an event looks exactly like that), not as a win.
- **interlock:** Requires network.carry_vce (hard error). carry_vce_apply_families must be a SUBSET of carry_vce_components (hard error, not a silent unity factor). Arming 'pl' on a lineage with phase_leveling off or carry_invariant on WARNS INERT — Card 4's DD carry supersedes the pl carry, so there is no fold site and no monitor row. Arming pb/cb WARNS UNVALIDATED (the pb mean/robust gap is ~200-1000x; SPEC section 8 defers both behind the pl gate). Never flip mid-chain; snapshot resume refused.

- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:1614  — **regime:** Mechanics field-validated over 13.2 h; POSTURE falsified. The user's own ruling stands: 'All guard knobs stay default-OFF.'

### `network.clock_disc_jump_test`
- **default:** include/config/GnssConfig.hpp (network_clock_disc_jump_test_{false}); parsed src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp (Card CLK-R stage 2, next to emit_clock_frame_hold)  — **regime:** Event-day / onset-enrichment studies ONLY.  Quiet-day arming is measured net-harmful at the live consumer.
- **evidence:** docs/SPEC_clock_emit_rebase.md §5/§6, runs/clkr_12h_{on,hold}: the mechanism is fully proven — 68 per-sat common-removed steps > 1.5 m (≈16 σ of the measured walk step) tie out event-row ↔ R record ↔ counters ↔ consumer reactions (+36 PB / +16 clk / +38 CB in replay), landing at genuine re-realizations.  But the marked population is ~98 % estimator churn (project_fast_clock_card), and resetting a converged float consumer on churn is pure transient cost: the live 12 h A/B paid p95 14.6 → 21.3 m and 1 → 106 epochs > 20 m (sustained +5–10 m up-bias windows), attributed ENTIRELY to the marks by the one-knob bisection (hold-only arm clean).  The edge-queue §1.4 onset-enrichment table is the pre-registered success criterion for any future arming.
- **interlock:** Independent of emit_clock_frame_hold (alone it marks the RAW wire with the census's own common-removed statistic).  Loader warns below the 0.5 m census floor (marks the walk itself, ~70+/h).  Routes through markSatDiscontinuity ⇒ bumps the sat's phase AND code AND clock counters (whole-sat semantics, R record).

### `network.clock_model (two-state oscillator carry)`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:1695 (false)
- **evidence:** NO-GO, closed with a calibration measurement. project_clock_model_hadamard_calib.md: 'Measured p ~ 1.0 (GPS 1.02 / Galileo 1.05) across tau in [30, 1800] s => white-FM / measurement dominated. RWFM q2 undetectable: GPS <= 8.7e-9, Galileo <= 2.7e-8 — ~17x BELOW the documented 1.5e-7 ... Intrinsic q2 upper bounds GPS <= 5.8e-12, Gal <= 1.7e-13 — 4-6 orders below 1.5e-7.' Consequence: 'Under WFM the two-state prediction sigma ~ sqrt(q1*dt) EQUALS the scalar carry ... and the mis-sized q gives a clock-monitor NIS-flag STORM.' Second, independent defect from project_network_ssystem_dia_assessment.md #2: 'Clock-model data-incest — the 2-state oscillator prediction is folded as a prior and its posterior fed back as a measurement with fixed R=0.05 m => NIS deflated in exactly the weak-data regime it is supposed to police. Fix this BEFORE arming network.clock_model in any shipped config.'
- **interlock:** DANGER: network.service_mode FORCE-ARMS it (armBool at :3062) — a service_mode run silently enables a measured NO-GO. It also makes filter.sat_clock_q a silent no-op and makes emit_clock_rate inert (clock_model wins). Its q1 default 0.0 / q2 1.5e-7 are the documented-but-falsified values. Run scripts/clock_hadamard_calib.py and check p before any arming.

### `network.cycle_slip.oracle_relevel`  (+ `_truth_root`, `_noise_cyc`, `_false_alarm`, `_window_s`)
- **default:** `src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:4151-4190` (false; `_truth_root ""`, `_noise_cyc 0.0`, `_false_alarm "keep"`, `_window_s 90.0`); mechanism `include/slip/NetworkSlipOracle.hpp` + `src/core/slip/NetworkSlipOracle.cpp`; actuation at the four detector refloat sites in `src/core/slip/NetworkSlipQc.cpp`; run-end counters `src/core/filter/NetworkTeardown.cpp:2532`  — **regime:** `on("network.phase_leveling") and not on("network.carry_invariant")`. **SIM-ONLY DIAGNOSTIC — a truth-fed response. Never a posture, never a golden.** It answers ONE question: what is the CEILING of a level-preserving slip response, so the engineering card (EWL / WL-MW jump estimation, `docs/SPEC_lane_slip_response.md`) can be priced before it is built. When a detector actuates a refloat on a CARRIED arc the true integer jump is read from the fixture's `truth_slips.csv` and `LocalCarry::value_abs` is SHIFTED by exactly Σδ·λ instead of freeing the arc; the gap re-entry drain and the T4 attribution erase are deliberately UNCHANGED, so the arm isolates a perfect DETECTOR response. **FIELD-INERT by construction** — a field lineage has no truth root, so the loader hard-errors. Contract: `docs/SPEC_slip_qc_hardening.md` §13; results `docs/SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount.md` §6.17e.
- **evidence:** Four arms on the slipped 60-station campaign bed (`runs/simnet60_campaign_wu93`, 2 452 truth slips / 6 h), 2026-09-10, all exit 0, OFF twin byte-identical (`D8D94923…`). **The mechanism is exact:** 1 161 repairs, |δ| p50 50 cyc, latency 0 on 1 124 of 1 161, **unrepaired fraction |e(t₀)−e(t₀−30)|/|δ| p50 0.005** (0.239 cyc), |e(t₀)|/|δ| **0.070** holding at 0.078 after 150 s. **Every pre-registered gate FAILS.** E0 → E0+ORACLE: ZWD settled truth rms **2.098 → 1.779 m** (target ≤ 0.5), ambient per-band DD |e| **1.65 → 2.43 cyc (WORSE)**, T4 `attrib_arc` **7 656 → 13 562 (+77 %)**, detector re-levels 4 545 → 687 (−85 %), consumer settled p50 1.751 → 1.730 (flat), ref-free WL(1,2) flat at 81.5 %, sat-clock contrast 8.05 → 7.78 m. On the ZWD-in-block posture ZWD 1.188 → 1.104 m but the consumer p50 goes 1.084 → **2.234 (2.1× worse)**. **Adding the E1 T4 starve is worse still** (`runs/golden_sim_campaign{,_zwdblk}_oracle_e1`): ZWD **1.819 / 1.239 m**, consumer p50 3.781 / 3.334, ref-free WL **35.4 / 39.7 %**, ambient DD |e| 6.09 / 5.66 cyc — worse than **E1 alone** on every channel E1 improved (0.933 m / 2.559 / 65.4 %) — while sat-clock contrast (3.16 m) and code bias (0.36 m) keep E1's gain. ⚠ **The ceiling instrument itself IMPROVES there**, which removes the obvious objection: on an identical 13 485-actuation population the repairs go **1 161 → 1 549** and `no_carry` collapses **3 149 → 1 396** (`bound_birth` 1 041 → 273) — T4 was deleting the carry out from under a repair. Slip-side census of the oracle-alone arm: 47.3 % REPAIRED / 25.6 % detected-but-KEPT (an open window-or-keying defect that biases the ceiling DOWNWARD) / 21.2 % FREED with no carry / 5.9 % SILENT; of 2 697 `oracle_keep` rows, **0** are a carry re-born by a T4 erase (the `birth_s` floor working) and only **8** are genuine data-domain false alarms. Detection is flat at 94.0 % on every arm. **Reading:** level preservation is the ceiling of HALF a fix — restored arcs are rejected by the corrupted majority, the per-band DD errors are equal in CYCLES while the lanes stay clean (ref-free L1C 0.896 / L2W 1.086 / L5Q 0.980 against EWL 0.032, WL 0.141), and solving `δρ + γ_b δI = ε λ_b` on the L1/L2 pair gives (0.1069, 0.0834)·ε m and predicts L5's own error to 0.6 %: a self-consistent **(range, iono) SPLIT** frozen into the zero-q levels, which no per-arc response can undo (§7 items 29 / 33; item 22 superseded).
- **interlock:** **Warn-and-DISARM** (not warn-only) under `network.carry_invariant` and without `network.phase_leveling` — armed-and-inert is forbidden for a knob whose whole output is a measured ceiling. HARD ERROR at load on a missing/unreadable `oracle_truth_root`, an unknown `oracle_false_alarm`, or a negative `oracle_noise_cyc`; HARD ERROR at setup when any ROSTER station lacks `truth_slips.csv` (a sim fixture writes one per station, so a missing file is a mis-pointed root, not a quiet station). `oracle_window_s < 0` clamps to 90 s with a warn. **Not fingerprinted** — it cannot be armed on a lineage with a snapshot worth resuming. Window = `( max(last_applied_s, LocalCarry::birth_s, now_s − window_s), now_s ]`; ⚠ `LocalCarry::last_seen_s` is NOT usable as the lower bound (it is the previous epoch's stash time and a zero-q level is not re-set by an intervening solve — using it read the normal population as false alarms, 701 kept against 88 repaired on a 60-epoch smoke). Run-end closure asserted (`seen == applied + fa_kept + fa_freed + no_carry + no_lambda`) plus **which lower bound was BINDING** — without that a too-narrow window and a genuinely quiet population are indistinguishable and they have opposite fixes. 19 unit cases `tests/test_slip_oracle.cpp`.

### `network.emit_pb_gauge_level`  (+ `_alpha`, `_min_stations`, `_min_concentration`, `_trace`, `_mode`)
- **default:** ⚠ **NOT IN `main`** — branch `worktree-agent-a7e2586003a605548`, commit `9b59a2c`, **not merged**. On that branch: parsed beside the `emit_pb_filter` block in `GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp` (false; `_alpha 0.2`, `_min_stations 5`, `_min_concentration 0.6`, `_trace false`, `_mode "level"`), applied in the P-record writer, trace `logs/network_pb_gauge.csv`. A `main`-tree config carrying these keys is silently ignored — verify against the branch before quoting any run as armed.  — **regime:** `on("network.phase_leveling")` (the innovation is the between-station spread of the CARRIED per-station SD fractions). **FALSIFIED AS BUILT (level mode) 2026-09-10 — do not merge, do not arm.**
- **evidence:** Single-knob 24 h A/B on the quiet truth bed (`runs/golden_sim_pbgauge` vs `_ctl`, fixture `runs/simnet60_quiet_wu93_24h`, 61 sta, 0 truth slips, exe `2AEC2EE4…`, both exit 0, 457 392 P records, consumer `hold_variance` 1e-6; graded with `scripts/simnet_wire_integer_consistency.py` + `scripts/consumer_float_truth_join.py`). **The mechanism actuated** — 347 683 P records adjusted (76.0 %), 143 families, mean |δ| 0.6505 cyc (P1 PASS) — **and changed nothing it exists to change.** G1 wire SD fraction p50|f| 0.25 → ≤ 0.08 on every band **FAIL, 0 of 12 channels** (ON wins 6, loses 6; the six primary bands 0.228–0.280, i.e. AT the uniform null). G2 consumer per-arc offset → ≤ 0.10 **FAIL, 1 of 12** (GPS EWL 0.173 → 0.036), 5 of 12 WORSE. G3 consumer fixes > 0 **FAIL — 0 fixes in BOTH arms**, ratio max 1.21 against a 3.0 threshold. G4 float consumer 3D rms ±10 % **FAIL — 0.0761 → 0.1037 m (+36 %)**, p50 +50 %. G5 PB-disc resets PASS (+1.7 %). It also made the wire NOISIER (within-tenure circular std ×1.1–1.9, tenure step up to ×2.1). **Two root causes, both general.** (1) **The premise is only weakly true:** the carried per-station SD fractions have R p50 **0.572** at n ≥ 5 against ≈ 0.41 for pure uniform noise at n = 6, and the carry covers a **median of 6 of 61 stations** — 39.0 % of innovations rejected `n<5`, 36.2 % `R<0.6`, only 24.9 % reach the EWMA. (2) **The unwrapped EWMA is a RANDOM WALK:** per-epoch |δ step| p99 0.039 cyc (inside the α/2 bound; 0.70 % of epochs above 0.05) but per-family |δ| span p50 **1.146 cyc**, max **7.67** — half of all families walk more than a full cycle in 24 h, and G03/L1C runs −0.06…−0.08 cyc/epoch for six hours under ONE unbroken reference (G09, 0 switches) to −7.63 cyc = **−1.45 m injected into the emitted L1C bias**, uncorrelated with PHASE_ANCHOR churn (20.18 events/sat on the big-δ families vs 20.78 elsewhere) — an integrator property, not an event-response failure. The deliberate "never WRAPS" choice, which correctly avoids tripping the emitter's discontinuity test, is exactly what turns a drifting fraction into an unbounded metre-scale ramp with no counter that fires. **Lesson:** an emission-side gauge estimate needs a BOUNDED/wrapped δ̂ with per-epoch authority and a much higher concentration bar — and even a perfect one could not have been exploited here, because the consumer's own float is 13.8 cyc sd and its AR dies at the ratio battery in 90 % of epochs. The correct answer to the wire's uniform fractions is the design doc's **Phase 3** (integer pins from a network DD fix), not an emission-side estimate — `docs/SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount.md` §6.17f, §7 item 30. ★ **THE CONTINUITY VARIANT (`_mode: "continuity"`) — FIELD ×3 + QUIET SIM, 2026-09-10 afternoon (§6.18d, §7 item 36).** This row's verdict is unchanged and the continuity variant is **also KEEP_OFF as a promotion**, for a different reason than the level mode: the mechanism does exactly what it claims and the defect is the **TRIGGER**. **The step-suppression half passes everywhere** — consumer PB-disc resets **−91 %** (field v1 diagonal, 1 158 → 104), **−94.7 %** (field v1 in-block, 2 322 → 122), **−93.2 %** (field v2) and **−93.4 %** on the quiet sim (2 274 → 151); the emitter's own |ΔP| > 0.25 λ jump test −97.6 / −98.8 / −97.4 %; unmarked `pb_L1C` step rate 2.925 → **0.074** /sat/h in-block; **emission-only by proof on every arm** (all "P," lines stripped ⇒ byte-identical products, `590470b2…` on the in-block trio, max |ΔC| = 0.0 EXACTLY). On the correct in-block posture v1 also takes the field consumer's **UP rms 1.862 → 1.131 m (−39 %)** (UP sd 1.127, gate ≤ 1.2 PASS), E 1.364 → 1.229 — the field vertical chain reads `on2` 3.71 → in-block 1.86 → + continuity **1.13 m**. **What blocks it is the trigger, measured:** `dev()` is linear, so dev(arm) = dev(ctl) + dev(STAIRCASE) = 0.7913 + **0.5314** = 1.1425 m on the settled pc_IF channel; the staircase fires **14 887 times** in the settled window at one-epoch |jump| p50 **0.026 m** against a 0.10-cycle trigger of ≈ 1.9 cm (~23 firings per family per hour), each a PERMANENT offset ⇒ the emitted level RANDOM-WALKS (p50 **18.8 m** of accumulated per-satellite excursion in 6 h). So G3 FAILS both halves in-block (pc_IF deviation 0.464 → 0.591 m, WORSE than its own control; step share 69.2 → 70.2 %) and the consumer's N rises **+25 %** on a near-horizontal due-south channel 84° off the geometric null. A datum artefact was tested and REJECTED (the per-epoch trim datum is QUIETER in the arm). **v2 pend-and-confirm is a REGRESSION** (3D 2.93 → **4.47 m**, UP rms 3.46 with a **−0.921 m/h** ramp, G3 0.844) and not for a tuning reason: the emitted P offset equals `delta_cont_cyc` EXACTLY on every row **including the 42 386 PENDING rows** (p50 = p99 = max = 0.000000), **96.8 % of the offset motion happens on non-confirmation rows**, 68 % of confirmations are force-resolved at the 13-epoch rail, and the (correct) revert path fires on 4.1 % of pends — it gated the LABEL, not the WIRE. ★ **The walk is a property of the TRIGGER, not of the field's event content:** on the quiet sim with ZERO truth slips the accumulated |δ_cont| still reaches p50 **28.6 cyc = 5.4 m** on L1 over 24 h (max 181 cyc), ~9× slower per hour than the field's only because **96.2 % of its candidates are sub-min against 72.3 % on the field**. That sim arm is otherwise clean — wire SD fractions unmoved (mean |p50|f| − 0.25| 0.0257 in BOTH arms: no integer content), code-bias/clock disc lines identical, AR unchanged, products byte-identical outside P, and the cold-handover population reconciles to the row (53 absorbed steps at `PBGAUGE|handover_cold`, p50 16.171 cyc, + 70 continuous = the clause's "123 at handovers") — at a price that is entirely tail (3D rms 0.0761 → 0.0932 m, **p50 unchanged** 0.0603 → 0.0601, p95 +43 %). ⚠ **The pair-1 trap** (`feedback_branch_exe_unknown_knob_silent`): the first field pair ran the DIAGONAL ZWD carry because the branch predated `966d4fd` and the loader silently ignored the key it did not know; the tell was `products.txt` `95afa6d9…` ≠ the main-tree `c48b9a5c…`, and pair 2's `logctl2` reproducing `c48b9a5c…` exactly is what certifies the rebased exe. Pair 1's "continuity converts jitter to wander and doubles the ZWD coupling" reading is POSTURE-SPECIFIC and does not reproduce in-block. **Rider from the 2×2's control column:** the in-block ZWD posture itself doubles-to-quadruples the wire's phase-bias churn (resets 1 158 → 2 322, unmarked `pb_L1C` steps 0.671 → 2.925 /sat/h, pc_IF deviation 0.230 → 0.464 m) — the wire-side price of §6.16.7d's vertical, and its own card. Owed and untouched by v2: the **196 rail pass-throughs in 15 field epochs, 9 with no handover marker**, against 24 of 26 DECLARED on the quiet sim — an invariant-#7 FIELD defect, not a property of the gauge. **v3** (hold the EMISSION, not the label, for the confirmation window — or a running median of the recent per-family offsets, absorbing only the confirmed departure from it) is in build on the same branch.  ⚠ **AMENDED 2026-09-10 evening — THE EMISSION-SIDE CONTINUITY CLASS IS CLOSED, FALSIFIED AS A CLASS** (SPEC_amb_iono_split_overcount §6.19c; §7 item 36 closes).  A third continuity variant settles the question the first two left open ("the mechanism is right, the TRIGGER is wrong").  All three arms on the in-block field posture vs `runs/fldf3_on2_zwdblk_logctl2` (DOY-129, 63 sta, 12 h, WTZZ closed-loop consumer; `logctl2`'s `products.txt` `c48b9a5c…` reproduces `runs/fldf3_on2_zwdblk` exactly, so the branch exe is certified on this posture by the §7 item 38 hash rule), settled ≥ h6.  **CONTROL 3D 2.93 m / UP 1.86 / N 1.80 / E 1.36.**  **v1** absorb-every-step (`_pbcont2`): 3D 2.80, UP **1.13** (−39 %, its UP-sd gate PASSES), N 2.25 (+25 %), E 1.23; 34 870 absorptions; accumulated |offset| p50 **125 cyc**.  **v2** pend-and-confirm (`_pbcont3`, `525e85c`): 3D 4.47, UP 3.46 with a −0.92 m/h ramp, N 2.64; the implementation moved the offset on PENDING rows and 68 % of confirmations were force-resolved at the rail; offsets p50 **132 cyc**.  **v3** running-median k = 5 then absorb (`_pbcont4`, `ad0b1d0`): **33 403 absorptions against v1's 34 870 — it absorbed 96 % of the same steps**; consumer 3D **8.26 m**, UP 7.34 (mean −6.3 m), N 3.57, corr(UP, BSHM ZWD) −0.70, offsets p50 **124 cyc**.  ⭐ **The decisive reading: the field's per-epoch phase-bias motion is a RANDOM WALK (raw |Δ| p50 4 cm/epoch, heavy-tailed p90 0.41 m), not spikes over a flat level, and A MEDIAN CANNOT SEPARATE A WALK FROM STEPS.**  Three different triggers, three different consumers, ONE accumulated-offset p50 (125 / 132 / 124 cyc) — the signature of the SOURCE, not of any trigger.  Quiet-sim twins agree on a bed with ZERO truth slips: v1 cuts consumer PB-disc resets **−93 %** and costs the float consumer **+22 %** rms (a tail), offsets already 28.6 cyc median over 24 h; v2 confirmed 2 622 of 4 048 pends and still walked.  **Why: the wire's biases walk because the PRODUCER's phase-bias state walks** — the same frozen (ρ, I) split of §7 item 40 seen through the anchors (§6.18c).  Any emission-side absorber converts walk-steps into offset-steps and hands the consumer an INTEGRATED error; the consumer prefers the resets, because a reset flushes accumulated error and an offset does not.  **The fix belongs in the estimation, not at emission.**  Verdict unchanged and now FINAL: KEEP_OFF.  Branch `worktree-agent-a7e2586003a605548` (HEAD `ad0b1d0`, rebased on main `721dd39`) stays UNMERGED as the record.
- **interlock:** Branch-only (see default). Gated behind `network.phase_leveling`; `_min_stations` / `_min_concentration` are the two acceptance bars and `_trace` writes the acceptance census that reconstructs the run-end counters exactly. `_mode` selects `level` (**falsified**) vs **`continuity`** — a DIFFERENT mechanism that absorbs frame STEPS at handovers and mass refloats and carries **no integer content**; that variant is **PENDING FIELD VALIDATION** (arms `_fldf3_on2_zwdblk_pbcont` vs `_logctl`, pre-registered in §7 item 31) and the level mode's failure modes do not follow to it. Emission-side only — it does not touch the solve, and the products' R-record retro bumps were byte-identical across the A/B. ⚠ **Branch-only cuts BOTH ways, now with a measured cost:** `_mode` and the continuity sub-knobs (`cont_*`) live only on the branch, so a main-tree exe ignores them silently — and a BRANCH exe ignores every main-tree key the branch predates, which is how the first continuity pair ran the wrong ZWD vehicle. Before any arm on a non-main exe: `git merge-base --is-ancestor <commit-of-every-armed-knob> <branch>`, grep the smoke console for each armed knob's run-end clause, and require a log-only twin of a main-tree run to reproduce that run's `products.txt` sha256 exactly (§7 item 38).
### `network.estimate_glonass`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:994 (false)
- **evidence:** BLOCKED at scale. project_glonass_ifb_network.md: '305-station global network (2024-129, GPS+Gal+GLONASS, Release) FAILS ... the 1-hour run diverges ~38 min in at tow 261510: Galileo clock-delta rms steps 2.8 m -> 7-13 km and oscillates; all 21 Galileo clocks blow up SIMULTANEOUSLY (block collapse), worst emitted E14 ~ -12.4 km ... DECISIVE A/B: the same 305-station network GPS+Gal only is rock-stable across all 121 epochs.' The 3-station and 11-epoch results are the trap, not the evidence: 'the 3-station regional fixture and the 11-epoch smoke BOTH passed clean — a common-sky regional fixture cannot expose a conditioning cliff, and a smoke cannot expose a 38-minute one.' Above kNullSpaceTol, so 'the rank gate stays clean (gate_ok=1, declared_dim=3) and never sees it.'
- **interlock:** FAIL-LOUD prerequisites (all hard errors): GLONASS in processing.active_systems, a GLONASS processing.signal_priorities entry, and uncombined PPP-RTK. FORCES estimate_ifb + enable_glonass_ifb. HARD ERROR with estimate_sat_code_bias (GLONASS admission skips the GPS/Gal receiver DCB that W6 needs).
### `network.lane_carry.fold_blocked`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:389-390 (jval default false); struct include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp:265
- **evidence:** Explicit standing arming rule against it: memory/project_lane_fold_perf_lf1.md:18-23 — "fold_blocked is an EQUIVALENCE-CLASS change that has never been quality-graded in the field — its only evidence is dd-frac drift p95 0.011 / max 0.11 on ONE G04-G03 L5X component with no FFR integer flipped. Do NOT flip it on for a graded run." The perf win is real but purchased with exactly the change class that has burned this project: same file lines 25-30 — "the per-row 0.5(Pz+Pz^T) symmetrize in the lane primitives is NOT a bit-exact no-op... dropping it default-ON drifted the lane-on solution ~1e-4 by epoch 4, reproducing the recorded-hash failure" — and the symmetrize-hoist rides behind this very knob. Measured perf (50-sta/10-ep Release, same-build): applyLaneFix 223 ms -> 0 ms; applyLaneMeasurement 2667 -> 1076 ms. The 150-sta 30-ep circR/med|f| A/B and the 305-sta closer are UNRUN.
- **interlock:** Inert unless network.lane_carry.enable, which FAIL-LOUDS without both network.carry_invariant AND network.mw_wl.ewl_enable (GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:2722-2729). Deliberately excluded from the equivalence_shape / lane_joint discriminators, so a shape-keyed comparison will NOT flag that it changed.
### `network.parallel_reduce`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:528 (jval default false); struct include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp:401
- **evidence:** SUPERSEDED by parallel_carry and actively harmful to arm. GnssConfigTypes.hpp:393-399: "the reduce fan-out combines per-thread partials in a different summation grouping — reproducible for a fixed thread count but ULP-different... the network's ill-conditioning amplifies that to ~1e-5 in the products". project_perf_deterministic_parallel_seam.md:63-65: "NOT byte-identical; the network's ill-conditioning amplifies its ULP ~1e-15 -> ~1e-5 over 121 carried epochs." It buys strictly less than parallel_carry (2.13-2.45x) while silently disabling it. - **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:100 (false)

It also creates the documented mis-logging trap, project_network_clock_rate_c1.md: 'GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:1696-1706 logs the emit_clock_rate/clock_model interaction BEFORE service_mode armBool(network_clock_model_) at :3026 ... The load log affirmatively misreports the active mechanism.' Note the bundle is otherwise well-designed (it warns on every override and re-runs the two validations it would bypass) — the defect is that its membership list predates the clock measurements.

Arms: emit_clock_disc, emit_ura, ura_retro_bump, clock_model, phase_leveling, raim, raim_attribution, raim_within_epoch, per_const_withhold, emit_epoch_status_record, scoped_handover_response, emit_generation_id, serialize_disc_counters, serialize_sbasis_contract, product_flush_per_epoch; conditionally sticky_pivot + pivot_vanish_return_signal. ### `network.sh_gate_rms_max_tecu`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:1654-1655 (default 0.0 = inactive)  — **regime:** Subordinate to sh_health_gate; the ruled replacement metric (rms over degrees >= 1 as a ratio to a frozen converged median) is a DIFFERENT quantity that this knob does not express.
- **evidence:** project_day129_gate_policy: "GOTCHA D-G3: sh_gate_rms_max_tecu is an ABSOLUTE ceiling on rms_all (a00 included), not the ruled ratio on deg >= 1 — do not overload it." The cross-run calibration does not transfer: "medians agree 2.38 vs 2.90 across runs; robust sigma differs 6x so a z does NOT transfer." Prerequisite before any threshold is armed: "the counterfactual harness... simulates any candidate policy against both a converged run and a seam-bearing run — require that table before arming any gate threshold."
- **interlock:** Silently disarms the whole gate when 0.0 AND the a00 band is silenced (:1670-1675).
### `network.sh_health_gate`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:1650-1651 (default false)  — **regime:** Emission-only SH-field withhold gate. Falsified as currently specified; user ruling on record: "All guard knobs stay default-OFF."
- **evidence:** project_day129_gate_policy, measured on runs/day129_guards/leg01 (600 ep) vs runs/day129_cont/seg01: "The SH gate's a00 band violates standing invariant #5. a00 is the field's DC term and trades against the code-bias/DCB common mode, so its LEVEL is a property of convergence history. Two realizations of the same day differ by ~25 TECU... an absolute a00 band cannot be derived transferably: the reference band [11.9, 63.1] would withhold ~100% of the leg's late epochs." Lowering the low edge "only dies at ~ -30, which measurably LOSES the 20:47 event"; a trailing-window robust z is "inert (0 engagements) on a run that begins diverged". Also: "Degrade-instead-of-withhold is a DEADLOCK as wired" — NetworkSolPrep.cpp:140 early-returns on sh_gate_engaged while the URA-ramp lift (:400) and ++n_emitted (NetworkEmitStore.cpp:367) are both downstream, so arming warmup_ura_floor_m with this gate means the floor can never lift. Fix is specified (gate on field SHAPE, rms over degrees >=1 as a RATIO to a frozen converged-window median) but unbuilt.
- **interlock:** AUTO-DISABLED with a warn if estimate_iono_sh is off (:1665-1669) or if the a00 band is silenced AND sh_gate_rms_max_tecu is 0 (:1670-1675) — the latter now INVERTS under the D-G3 ruling (band-silenced is legitimate). Emission-only, so NOT in the resume fingerprint. GOTCHA on file: sh_gate_rms_max_tecu is an ABSOLUTE ceiling on rms_all (a00 included), not the ruled ratio on deg>=1 — do not overload it.

- **default:** include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp:726 (state_snapshot_keep_history{false}); parsed GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:704  — **regime:** Branch-grading diagnostic only (writes <path>.ep<NNNN> per snapshot, consumed by scripts/diff_carry_blobs.py). Arm for a blob-forensics session, never in production.

Write-side only; heavy on disk' (GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:711-714). Also: the branch-fidelity z metric it exists to feed is INVALID ACROSS RUNS — 'serialized z is the correction-frame DD (minus sum c*x_lin) => run-specific; its apparent decay tracks frame divergence, not branch loss', and the 0.05-cyc bar sits below the noise floor. So the primary consumer of this history is a metric that has been retired pending a frame-invariant functional.
- **interlock:** Write-side only; independent of state_snapshot_carry.

- **default:** include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp:712 (state_snapshot_resume_shock_m{0.0}); parsed GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:694  — **regime:** Falsified at every sigma tested {0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50} on the 50-sta GPS-only warm-resume A/B; explicitly marked do-not-re-propose.

It monotonically shrinks the transient AND erodes the long run (slope 30.2 -> 37.1 at 0.50 m). Decision tree => STOP; the follow-on increment was struck. Do not re-propose resume shock.'

- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoadNetwork.cpp:605 (jval default false); struct include/config/GnssConfigTypes.hpp:606
- **evidence:** Study-only instrument built for ONE open question (SPEC_day129_frame_guards P1-4, RHS localization: which stations / satellites / signals carry the misfit), and the user's own record puts it on the do-not-arm list for the live investigation chain: memory/project_day129_pivot_cb_ratchet.md:17-21 — "Guard build state — BUILT in the working tree, UNCOMMITTED, default-OFF, and they MUST STAY OFF: ... trace_postfit_keyed (:596)". ### `processing.eigen_threads`
- **default:** src/core/config/GnssConfigLoad.cpp:557 (jval default 1); ctor src/core/config/GnssConfig.cpp:125; getter include/config/GnssConfig.hpp:1221  — **regime:** Keep at 1. Raising it is measured-harmful for PPP/baseline sizes and essentially INERT for the parallel network path — it is not the network perf lever it looks like.
- **evidence:** Application.cpp:1437-1442: "PPP-RTK / baseline matrix sizes (n~350, m~250) sit below the size at which OpenMP fork/join overhead is offset by the parallel speedup — in profile traces vcomp140 idle-at-barrier showed up as ~13% of the run." For the network: ApplicationNetwork.cpp:745-746 pins Eigen to 1 for the WHOLE epoch loop whenever parallel_on, and every un-pin site (NetworkNormalEquations.cpp:926, NetworkWlRunTypes.hpp:135-138 via ApplicationNetwork.cpp:1485 and NetworkInstallCarry.cpp:141) restores n_threads, NOT eigen_threads. The 305 all-on config sets processing.eigen_threads=20 — harmless, but it cannot be credited with any of that run's 27.7 s/ep.
